The Whale's Lullaby (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

Deep in the blue, blue sea, where the coral glows like tiny stars, a great whale named Wally swam with a gentle hum. His song was soft as a feather, slow as a sleepy moon, and it carried all the way to a little shell.

Wally's hum went like this: Ooooh-ahhhh, ooooh-ahhhh, like a breeze through the seaweed, like a dream through the waves. And when he hummed, the tiny fish floated still, and the jellyfish pulsed in time, and the whole ocean seemed to sway, sway, sway.

One day, Wally noticed a tiny shell resting on a sandy bed, all alone and quiet. The shell shimmered faintly, like a pearl holding a heartbeat. Wally leaned close and whispered, "Little shell, are you listening?"

The shell gave a tiny shimmer, a little pulse of light. Wally smiled (as much as a whale can smile) and said, "I will sing for you, little one. I will sing a song of waiting and of love."

So Wally began his lullaby. Ooooh-ahhhh, ooooh-ahhhh, slow and deep, like a rocking cradle. He sang of the moon that rises over the waves, and of the stars that blink like friendly eyes. He sang of the gentle currents that carry tiny eggs to safe, warm places.

The little shell shimmered brighter, and Wally felt a tiny vibration, like a tiny heartbeat echoing inside. He sang on, "You are loved, little shell. You are safe, little shell. The ocean holds you, and I hold you in my song."

And as he sang, the sea grew calm, the waves smoothed like a silken blanket, and the moonlight spilled silver over the water. The tiny fish gathered in a circle, their scales twinkling like tiny candles, and they joined Wally's hum: Ooooh-ahhhh, ooooh-ahhhh.

The little shell pulsed in rhythm, and Wally knew that inside that shell, a tiny heart was listening, a tiny soul was dreaming. He sang until the stars began to yawn, and the moon tucked itself behind a pillow of clouds.

Then Wally gently nudged the shell with his great, soft nose, and said, "Goodnight, little one. I will be here, always, humming this song. When you are ready, you will hear it in your dreams."

And the little shell glowed one last time, like a tiny goodnight kiss, and the ocean hummed along with Wally's lullaby, a soft, endless, loving hum: Ooooh-ahhhh, ooooh-ahhhh, ooooh-ahhhh...

So if you ever listen to the sea, and you hear a deep, gentle hum, know that it is Wally the whale, singing a song of love to a little shell—and to you. Sleep now, little one, wrapped in the ocean's song.

✨ Story Lesson
Even the smallest and most distant listeners are wrapped in the love and guardianship of those who care for them.

2026年7月31日 · 星期五
云朵上的小摇篮（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

月亮妈妈轻轻摇着云朵摇篮，里面躺着一颗亮晶晶的小星星。风儿哼着歌，云朵软绵绵，小星星在梦里笑出了声。

云朵摇篮晃啊晃，像小船在水上飘。小星星眨眨眼，看见一朵小花从云缝里探出头来。小花说：「你好呀，小星星，我是云朵上的花，专门来陪你睡觉的。」

小星星伸个懒腰，发现摇篮边坐着一个小精灵，拿着小勺子，轻轻搅动着月光。小精灵说：「我在做星星汤，加了甜甜的梦，喝一口，就能梦见最漂亮的地方。」

小星星喝了一小口，觉得全身暖洋洋的。忽然，他看见一朵云变成了一只小兔子，蹦蹦跳跳地过来，说：「来吧，我带你去看云朵上的城市！」

小星星骑在小兔子的背上，飞过一片片云海。云朵上的城市有棉花糖做的房子，有彩虹搭的桥，还有会唱歌的喷泉。喷泉里流出来的不是水，是亮晶晶的星星碎片。

「这是哪里呀？」小星星好奇地问。小兔子说：「这是云朵上的梦之城，每个宝宝睡着的时候，都会来这里玩耍。」

小星星玩着玩着，忽然听见远处传来好听的歌声。那是妈妈的声音，像春风一样温柔，轻轻唱着：「小星星，亮晶晶，妈妈怀里好安宁。」

小星星听了，心里暖暖的，他对小兔子说：「我想妈妈了，我要回我的小摇篮去。」

小兔子点点头，轻轻一蹦，就把他送回了云朵摇篮里。月亮妈妈还在那儿，用温柔的手轻轻拍着他的背。

小星星躺回摇篮里，闭上眼睛，听着妈妈的声音，慢慢进入了甜甜的梦乡。云朵摇篮还在晃啊晃，风儿还在哼着歌，一切都那么安静，那么美好。

✨ 故事小语
每个宝宝都是妈妈心里最亮的那颗星，被爱轻轻包裹着，安心地长大。

The Little Baker's First Loaf (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

In a cozy little town, there lived a tiny baker named Bella, who woke up with the sun to make the very first loaf of bread. She hummed a soft, slow tune as she mixed the flour and water, just like her mama taught her.

Slosh, slosh, went the water. Swish, swish, went the spoon. Bella stirred and stirred, until the dough was as smooth as a lullaby.

Then she sprinkled in a pinch of salt, a dash of sugar, and a secret whisper of love. Knead, knead, knead, she pressed and folded, and the dough seemed to sigh with happiness.

Now, the dough needed to rest. So Bella placed it in a warm spot by the window, and she waited. She watched the clouds drift by, and she listened to the breeze whistle, hoo-hoo, hoo-hoo, and she thought of all the little seeds waiting to grow.

After a long, patient wait, the dough began to rise. It puffed up, soft and round, like a sleepy bear waking from a nap. Bella clapped her hands, and oh, what a wonderful sight!

Then it was time for the oven. Bella slid the dough in with a gentle shove, and she sat down to wait some more. The kitchen filled with a warm, golden smell, and Bella's tummy rumbled, rumble-rumble, like a tiny drum.

Finally, the timer went ding! Bella opened the oven, and there it was—a beautiful, golden-brown loaf, steaming and singing a happy song. She pulled it out, let it cool on a rack, and then she cut a slice, just one, to taste.

The bread was soft and sweet, with a crunchy crust that crackled like autumn leaves. Bella smiled, and she knew that all the waiting and stirring and kneading had made something truly wonderful.

She wrapped the rest in a warm cloth and decided to share it with everyone in her little town. Because when you bake with patience and love, there's always enough to share.

And as the moon rose high, Bella tucked herself into bed, dreaming of flour clouds and doughy stars. Goodnight, little baker. Goodnight, little one. Sleep tight, and dream sweet.

✨ Story Lesson
Patience and love make everything turn out just right.

2026年8月1日 · 星期六
月亮妈妈的小月饼（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

月亮妈妈在夜空中轻轻摇啊摇，手里捧着一块亮晶晶的小月饼。云朵宝宝们围过来，好奇地问：'月亮妈妈，你做的月饼是什么味道呀？'

月亮妈妈笑眯眯地说：'这是用星星的甜和露珠的凉做的，还有一点点桂花的香。'她轻轻咬了一小口，'嗯——软软的，像妈妈的手。'

这时候，风宝宝呼呼地跑过来，卷起一片银光：'月亮妈妈，月亮妈妈，你的月饼里有没有藏着小秘密？'

'有呀，'月亮妈妈眨眨眼睛，'里面藏着一句悄悄话：宝贝，别急，慢慢长。'

她说着，把月饼掰成一小块一小块，撒向大地。那些碎屑变成了闪闪的月光，落在小床上、小窗边，还有妈妈轻轻哼歌的地方。

看，小宝宝在梦里翻了个身，好像听见了月亮妈妈的声音。月光轻轻拍着他，像摇篮一样晃啊晃。

月亮妈妈守了一整夜，直到天边露出淡淡的橘色。她悄悄收起剩下的月光，说：'明天，我再带新的月饼来。'

夜静悄悄的，只有心跳声和呼吸声，像在说：'慢慢来，我们在等你。'

✨ 故事小语
爱和守护就像月光一样，温柔地包围着我们，等待新生命慢慢长大。

The Little Star's Treasure Hunt (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

Softly, softly, a little star twinkled high above the sleepy world. It whispered to the moon, 'I want to find a treasure, a special one, before the night is through.' The moon smiled a gentle silver smile and said, 'Little star, the treasure you seek is not far away. Follow the quiet breeze that hums a lullaby, and let it guide you on your way.'

So the little star drifted down, down, down, through the velvet sky, past clouds as soft as sheep's wool, floating slowly, gently by. The breeze sang a soft 'shhhh, shhhh' as it danced among the leaves. 'Follow me,' it seemed to say, 'to a secret place of ease.'

Down in a cozy meadow, where the grass was soft and deep, a mother rabbit hummed a tune to her babies, fast asleep. 'Shhh,' she said, 'my little ones, the night is calm and still. The stars are watching over us, and all the world is still.' The little star peeked through the grass and saw the baby bunnies snuggle, their noses twitching, ears so soft, all warm inside their huddle.

The breeze then tickled a sleepy stream that sang a 'gurgle, gurgle, plip.' 'Come this way,' the water whispered, 'I know a treasure fit.' The star followed, past a sleeping owl, who hooted a soft 'too-whoo.' 'The treasure's nearly found now, just a little further for you.'

At last the breeze grew very still, and the star saw a sight so dear: a little bird in a nest so warm, with a tiny golden tear. 'Oh, hello there,' the birdie chirped, 'I lost my shiny bead. It rolled away into the dark, when I had so much need.' The star twinkled bright and said, 'Don't cry, I'll help you find it, you and I.'

They searched beneath a mushroom cap, and under a leafy bed. The star glowed softly, 'There! I see it,' as the bead shone up ahead. The little bird gave a happy 'tweet,' and hopped with joy to see. 'You found my treasure, lovely star! Now will you stay with me?'

The star smiled down and said, 'My treasure was to help you, dear. For the greatest treasure in the world is to bring a friend a cheer.' The bird and star then sang a song, a soft and sleepy tune, and the star floated up, up, up, back to the silver moon.

And as the star settled in the sky, it looked down at the nest so sweet. The bird was sleeping, bead in wing, with a happy little beat. The star whispered to the moon, 'I found my treasure, true.' And the moon replied, 'Yes, little one, the treasure was you.'

So close your eyes, little one, and listen to the breeze. The world is full of gentle joys, and you are loved with ease. Shhh, shhh, the night is soft, the stars are twinkling high. Sleep tight, my dear, and dream of treasure, beneath the quiet sky.

✨ Story Lesson
The greatest treasure is the love we carry and share with others.

2026年8月2日 · 星期日
妈妈的摇篮曲（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

月亮婆婆轻轻晃，星星宝宝眨眼睛。妈妈的手放在肚子上，唱起软软的摇篮曲。

小宝宝在妈妈肚子里，像一条小鱼游啊游，游进温暖的梦里。

妈妈唱：'睡吧，睡吧，我亲爱的宝贝，妈妈的怀抱，是甜甜的港湾。'

宝宝听见了，小脚丫轻轻踢一踢，好像在说：'妈妈，我在这里呀。'

爸爸也来了，他贴着妈妈的肚子，小声说：'小宝贝，我是爸爸，等你长大，我们一起去数星星。'

宝宝听见了，小手手挥一挥，好像在说：'爸爸，我记住啦。'

风儿轻轻吹，窗帘慢慢摇，妈妈的声音像羽毛，痒痒的，暖暖的。

宝宝在肚子里，翻了个身，蜷成一个小圆球，像一颗小种子，躲在泥土里，等着春天发芽。

妈妈闭上眼，心里轻轻说：'宝贝，我们等你，等了好久好久。'

月光洒进窗子，照在妈妈的笑脸上，宝宝在梦里，也笑了。

整个世界都安静了，只有妈妈的心跳，扑通，扑通，像一首最古老的歌。

宝宝听着听着，睡得香香的，在妈妈的爱里，慢慢长大。

✨ 故事小语
宝宝在妈妈肚子里，听着妈妈的心跳和歌声，感受到满满的爱与等待。

2026年8月3日 · 星期一
小月亮的守夜灯（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

夜晚来了，天黑黑的，可天上亮起了一盏一盏的小灯——那是星星。最圆最亮的那一盏，是小月亮。小月亮提着一盏柔柔的守夜灯，慢慢地，在夜空里走。

小月亮走到窗前，看见屋子里有一位妈妈，正轻轻拍着一个圆圆的小肚子。妈妈的声音软软的：「宝贝呀，天上的小月亮在守着你呢。」小月亮听见了，把灯举得低低的，让光轻轻落在窗台上。

风来了，风把小月亮的灯吹得晃了晃。小月亮不怕，她用云朵把灯裹住，光就变成暖暖的一团。她说：「风儿别急，我陪着这个家呢。」

一只小萤火虫飞上来，绕着灯转圈圈。小月亮笑着：「你也有一盏小灯呀。」萤火虫说：「我是来跟你做伴的。」于是夜空里，有一盏大的、一盏小的，一闪一闪，像在说悄悄话。

小宝宝还在妈妈肚子里，可他好像听见了。他在温水里伸了个懒腰，小脚丫轻轻顶了顶妈妈的肚子。妈妈笑了：「你看，宝宝在跟你打招呼呢。」

小月亮把守夜灯挂在窗外的树枝上，自己悄悄退到云后面。她说：「我走了，我的光留下来。」那盏灯的光，变成了窗台上一小片银银的亮。

夜深了，妈妈哼起了歌。小月亮在云后听着，觉得心里也暖暖的。她知道，每一个等待的宝宝，都有一盏看不见的灯在守着——那是妈妈的爱，比月光还要亮。

小萤火虫也累了，趴在灯边睡了。夜安静下来，只有妈妈的呼吸，和小宝宝轻轻的心跳，扑通，扑通，像在和月光一起数星星。

天快亮的时候，小月亮提着灯，慢慢往天上走。她回头看了一眼那扇窗：灯还亮着，小宝宝还在甜甜的梦里。「明天晚上，我再来。」小月亮轻轻地说。

宝宝呀，你还没来到这个世界上，可已经有一盏小月亮的守夜灯，天天为你亮着。等你来了，记得抬头看看夜空——那最亮的一盏，是在对你笑呢。

✨ 故事小语
你还没来到这个世界，就已经被温柔地守着了。妈妈的等待，像小月亮的守夜灯，天天为你亮着——你从来都不是一个人。

The Little Seed Who Waited (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

A tiny seed lay in the dark, dark earth. Wait, wait, wait, said the earth. The seed was snug and warm. Shhh, said the rain. The seed listened.

Up above, a mama sang a soft, soft song. Rock, rock, went her chair. Hush, little seed, the song seemed to say. The seed smiled in the dark. I am waiting, it whispered.

Then the sun came, warm and gold. Knock, knock, said the sun. The seed stretched a tiny root down, down, down. Wiggle, wiggle, went the root. The earth hugged it close.

Then a small green tip pushed up, up, up. Peek, peek, said the world. The seed opened two little leaves. Oh, said the wind. You are awake!

The mama looked down and smiled. Her hand rested on her round, round tummy. The seed did not know, but the mama's song had kept it safe all along.

A small bird sang tweet, tweet above the leaf. The seed swayed, happy and new. The mama hummed her song again: wait, little one, wait and grow.

Night came. The moon poured silver on the leaf. The seed slept, leaf and root together, in the warm, warm dark. The mama's heartbeat went thump, thump, like a quiet drum.

Far away, a little star blinked: I see you, small green one. The seed dreamed of sunshine and rain and a mama's song. Grow, grow, whispered the earth.

Morning came again. The leaf caught a drop of dew. The mama laughed softly: look at you, so brave. The seed turned its face to the sun.

So if you wait, little one, and you grow, the whole world waits with you. The earth, the rain, the sun, and a mama's love — all saying: we are here. Sleep now, small green one, and dream of light.

✨ Story Lesson
When you wait and you grow, the whole world grows with you — the earth, the rain, the sun, and a mama's love, all saying: we are here.

2026年8月4日 · 星期二
小熊的安心被窝（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

森林里下起了细细的雨。小熊躲进树洞里，抱着自己软软的尾巴。雨滴答，滴答，敲着树叶的小鼓。小熊有点想妈妈了。

熊妈妈走过来，把一件蓬蓬的被子盖在小熊身上。「别怕，」熊妈妈的声音像热汤一样暖，「雨是来给小河喂水的，不是来吓你的。」小熊闻着妈妈身上的松木香，慢慢不抖了。

洞外，风呼呼地跑过，把一片叶子贴在洞口。小熊问：「风会不会进来？」熊妈妈笑了：「风只是路过，它知道这里住着我的小宝贝，会轻轻绕开。」

小熊把爪子伸进被子里，摸到妈妈缝的一颗小星星扣子。那是妈妈用旧毛衣上的纽扣缝的，说：「想妈妈的时候，就摸摸这颗星星。」小熊摸了摸，心里亮了一下。

雨小了。小熊听见远处有谁在哼歌——是夜莺在雨后的树枝上，唱着软软的调子。「你听，」熊妈妈说，「森林里每个人都在陪你。」小熊点点头，把下巴搁在爪子上。

小宝宝也在妈妈的肚子里呢，小熊想。他是不是也有一床安心的被子？熊妈妈好像听见了他的心思，说：「每个宝宝都有呀。妈妈的肚子里，又暖又软，像一床一直盖着的被子。」

小熊打了个哈欠。眼皮沉甸甸的，像挂着两片小叶子。熊妈妈轻轻拍着他的背：「睡吧，雨会停，天会亮，妈妈一直在这儿。」

洞外，雨真的停了。月亮从云缝里探出头，把银银的光洒在小熊的被子上。小熊在梦里，看见自己和宝宝一起，躺在一床大大的、软软的安心里。

森林睡了。只有溪水还在轻轻流，像在替熊妈妈，一下一下，拍着全世界的小宝贝。晚安，小熊。晚安，每一个被安心包围的小生命。

✨ 故事小语
害怕的时候，记得有人一直守着你。妈妈的怀抱，就是一床永远盖着的安心被子——你从来都被暖暖地包着。

The Cloud Who Couldn't Sleep (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

High up where the sleepy clouds go, one little cloud named Pip said, 'No! I will not fluff, I will not drift, I cannot seem to fall asleep a bit!'

Pip puffed and Pip spun, Pip zigged and Pip zun. The stars leaned down: 'Little cloud, what's wrong?' Pip sighed a soft sigh, 'My dreams have all gone!'

Then a old moon with a silver spoon said, 'Come, come, we'll hum a tune.' He rocked the cloud, swish, swish, slow. The wind sang low, 'Oooo, oooo, don't go.'

A tiny star dropped a kiss of light, 'Here, Pip, hold this through the night.' Pip curled round the glow, warm and small, and the moon kept humming, one and all.

Below, a mama on the ground, with a round tummy, made not a sound. She hummed a tune Pip could almost hear: 'Sleep, little cloud, your dreams are near.'

Pip floated soft, like a feather on air. The thunder gave up its grumbly stare. The lightning dimmed to a blink, blink, blink, and the night turned the color of sleepy pink.

A small rain fell, pitter, pat, but it landed like love where the dream-catchers sat. Pip yawned a yawn, wide and slow: 'Maybe my dreams were here, just slow.'

The mama below touched her tummy so round, and the baby within gave a tiny turn-round. Pip watched from above with a sleepy little grin: 'That's a dream, that's a dream, tucked softly within!'

So if you can't sleep, little one, don't you weep. A moon, a star, and a mama's hum keep, ALL will rock you, fluff and deep. Shhh, said Pip, and Pip fell asleep.

✨ Story Lesson
When you can't sleep, you are not alone — a moon, a star, and a mama's hum are all rocking you, soft and deep.

✦ ✦ ✦
2026年8月5日 · 星期三
云朵上的小邮局（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

有一天，风儿送来一封皱皱的信，落在宝宝的小窗台上。信上画着一朵云，云上写着：「欢迎来到云朵小邮局！」小当当踮起脚，把信贴在了宝宝的小床边。

云朵小邮局开在天空最高的那朵棉花糖云上。邮差是一只长着翅膀的小刺猬，名字叫绒绒。绒绒每天背着一个小布袋，里面装的全是「还没说出口的话」。

第一封信，是远方的外婆寄来的：「宝贝呀，外婆给你织了一件云朵小毛衣，软软的，等你来了就穿上。」绒绒把信折成一只小纸鹤，让它飞进宝宝的梦里。

第二封信，是院子里的老槐树寄来的：「我攒了一夏天的阴凉，都给你留着。等你来了，我在树下给你讲去年的故事。」小当当听了，胸口白毛一翘一翘：「宝宝，老槐树也想你呢。」

第三封信，是还没出生的你寄来的——对呀，你也会寄信！信上画着一个圆圆的小拳头，写着：「妈妈，我听见你的心跳了，像鼓一样，咚咚咚，我喜欢。」

绒绒把这三封信，轻轻放进宝宝的梦里。梦里，外婆的毛衣暖暖的，老槐树的阴凉凉凉的，你的小拳头软软的。宝宝翻了个身，嘴角弯成月牙。

小当当趴在宝宝床边，把胸口白毛贴着小床，咕噜咕噜：「宝宝，你看，全世界都在给你写信呢。」三只猫挤成一团，把梦守得严严实实。

云朵小邮局打烊了。绒绒把最后一盏灯拧小，云慢慢飘远。风儿把信箱的盖子轻轻合上，说：「明天还有新的信呢。」

宝宝呀，你还没出生，就已经收到好多好多的信。每一封，都是有人想你、等你、爱你的证明。等你来了，这些信，会变成真真实实的拥抱。

夜深了。窗台上的信纸被月光照得发亮。小当当把下巴搁在爪子上，轻轻哼起歌——云朵小邮局的灯，还亮着一盏，留给每一个还没来的小宝贝。

✨ 故事小语
你还没出生，就已经收到全世界寄来的信——每一封，都是有人想你、等你、爱你的证明。等你来了，这些信会变成真真实实的拥抱。

The Starfish Who Counted Stars (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

Down by the quiet shore, where the sand is soft and the tide goes shhh, a little starfish named Stella lay on a smooth, flat stone. Stella had five tiny arms and one big wish: to count the stars.

One, said Stella, as a star blinked above the waves. Two, said Stella, as another twinkle came. Three, four, five — the stars kept popping, like fireflies someone had tossed in the sky.

A gentle crab named Click stepped close. Click, click, went his legs on the stone. 'Stars are too many,' he said. 'You will count all night!' Stella smiled: 'Then I will count with the moon as my light.'

Far away, in a cozy room, a mama hummed a song to her round, round tummy. The baby inside gave a tiny kick, kick, soft as a pebble dropped in a pool. The mama laughed: 'Are you dancing, little star?'

Stella counted on, six, seven, eight. Each star she named felt like a friend. Nine, ten, eleven — the tide came in, cool and slow, and kissed the stone where Stella lay. 'Shhh,' said the sea, 'I am counting too.'

Twelve, thirteen, fourteen. Stella's arms grew sleepy. Fifteen, sixteen — the moon poured silver on the water, and the waves turned to liquid light. Stella yawned a wide, slow yawn.

Somewhere, a little whale sang ooooh-ahhhh, deep and low, the same song the mama hummed. Stella smiled: 'The whole world is humming one lullaby.' Seventeen, eighteen, nineteen...

Twenty, whispered Stella, and her eyes grew heavy. The stars above, the stars in the sea, the star in the mama's song — all twinkling together, counting the baby to sleep.

The tide went shhh, the crab went click, click, soft. Stella tucked her five arms in, and the moon pulled a cloud-blanket up to her chin. 'Goodnight, stars,' she sighed. 'Goodnight, little one.'

So if you look at the sea on a starry night, you may see a starfish asleep on a stone, still counting, still humming: one for you, one for me, all the stars are family.

✨ Story Lesson
The whole world shares one lullaby — the stars, the sea, and a mama's song all twinkle together, counting you to sleep. You are never outside the family of light.

✦ ✦ ✦
2026年8月5日 · 星期三
小海螺的第一次远行
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

在蔚蓝的大海深处，有一片轻轻摇曳的海草森林。

海草森林里，住着一只小小的白海螺。她的壳上，有一圈圈淡粉色的花纹，像极了傍晚天边的云霞。她是整片海草森林里最小的海螺，大家都亲切地叫她"小旋旋"。

小旋旋有一个愿望：她好想去看看海面上的世界。

海龟爷爷告诉她："海面上有光，有风，还有一种叫'月亮'的东西，圆圆亮亮的，比最大的珍珠还美。"

"可是，我游得这么慢……"小旋旋有些担心。

海马爸爸笑着说："没关系呀，大海会帮你。你听——"

就在这时，一股温柔的海流，像一条看不见的绸带，轻轻地托起了小旋旋。她没有用力，只是顺着海流，慢慢地、慢慢地向上升。

一路上，她看到了发光的蓝色水母，像一盏盏小小的灯；遇到了成群的小银鱼，它们排成队，像一条闪闪发亮的银河。每经过一个地方，小旋旋就在心里默默记住这片美景。

不知道漂了多久，海水变得越来越亮。

终于——"噗"，一个小小的浪花托起她，小旋旋第一次探出了海面。

她看到了什么？

深蓝色的天空中，挂着一轮圆圆的、洁白的月亮。月光洒在海面上，碎成千万片银色的光斑，随着波浪轻轻摇晃。风，是软软的，咸咸的，吹在她的小壳上，像一首没有歌词的摇篮曲。

"原来，这就是月亮啊。"小旋旋轻轻地感叹。

她觉得自己好幸福——不是因为她游得快，而是因为她相信大海，大海就把她带到了最美的地方。

就在这时，一阵更温柔的海流，从远处缓缓涌来。那海流不冷也不热，速度不快也不慢，像是在包裹着什么珍贵的东西。那是深海中最温暖、最安全的一股洋流，所有的海洋居民都知道——那是生命开始的洋流。

小旋旋顺着那股洋流望过去，看到很远很远的海域里，有一团小小的、暖暖的光。那光一明一暗，节奏平稳而有力，像一颗小小的心脏在跳动。

"那是什么？"小旋旋问。

月亮轻轻地回答："那是一个正在成长的小生命呀。像你一样，在最适合自己的'海洋'里，听着世界上最美妙的声音——妈妈的心跳。"

小旋旋把自己的壳轻轻贴在海水里，仿佛也听到了那来自远方的节奏："咚、咚、咚……"

那是勇敢的鼓点，也是爱的节拍。

"小宝宝，"小旋旋对着那团光轻轻地说，"等你准备好了，外面的世界有软软的风，有圆圆的月亮，还有整片大海等着拥抱你。现在，你只要安心长大就好。"

那团小小的光，似乎微微亮了一下，像是在回应。

小旋旋心满意足地沉回海草森林，在月光的轻抚下，进入了一个充满了温柔波浪的梦乡。

晚安，小海螺。

晚安，小宝宝。

✨ 故事小语
只要你愿意相信，世界会把你带到最美的地方；而最温柔的守护，是妈妈的心跳一直在等你。

The Red-Blood-Cell Courier (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

Deep, deep in the warm red sea, a tiny courier named Ruby rides a soft current. Her job is to carry a glowing light to every corner of the cozy world.

Whoosh, whoosh, she floats, a little red cell, round and bright, like a tiny lantern in the gentle dark. She carries a packet of fresh, sparkly air, wrapped in a bubble of light.

She passes by the bone family, who are having their night talk. Tap, tap, tap, they say. We are the strong pillars, we hold you up, we keep you safe. Ruby smiles and waves, leaving a little glow on their smooth white walls.

Then she meets the blinking windshield wiper, who lives near the eye's soft window. Swish, swish, it goes, keeping the view clear and clean. Ruby says, So that is how it is... you keep the world from getting blurry. The wiper blinks a friendly good-bye.

Ruby's favorite part is when she reaches the brain, all curled up like a sleepy kitten. The brain whispers, Shh... we are dreaming of soft clouds and round moons. Ruby leaves a tiny spark of light there, and the brain hums a gentle lullaby.

But the best moment comes when Ruby rides past the heart, the great drum. Boom-boom, boom-boom, it calls, steady and strong. Ruby stops and listens. The heart's drumbeat is the most beautiful sound in the whole red sea. It says, I am here, I am here, I will always keep you safe.

Little one, can you hear? That boom-boom is your own heartbeat, and it is the rhythm of love. It carries Ruby and all her friends on their endless, gentle journey.

And so, when you are ready, the soft wind and the round moon will be waiting. But for now, just grow safe and sound, and let the red-blood-cell courier ride on, carrying light, carrying love, carrying the wonderful secret that you are already so beautifully alive.

✨ Story Lesson
Every little part of you works with love, and your heartbeat is a song that keeps everything safe and bright.

小星星暖暖的奇妙发现（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

小星星暖暖圆滚滚的，像一颗亮晶晶的糯米团子，住在软软的云朵上。她最爱做的事，就是眨着眼睛往外看——云朵下面呀，有一个大大的、蓝蓝的世界。

有一天，暖暖正抱着云朵打盹儿，忽然听见一阵声音：咕咚咕咚……咕咚咕咚……那声音轻轻的、柔柔的，像谁在敲一面小鼓。暖暖竖起小耳朵，小声说：咦？这是什么声音呀？

她顺着声音飘呀飘，穿过一片棉花糖一样的云海。云海里有小小的水珠在跳舞，它们蹦蹦跳跳地说：咕咚咕咚，是妈妈的心跳呀！暖暖歪着头，好像有点明白了。

她继续往前飘，忽然看见一片金色的光晕，像一床软软的被子铺在水面上。暖暖轻轻落进去，呀，好暖和呀！那光晕像摇篮一样，一摇一摇的，把暖暖裹得紧紧的。暖暖闭上眼睛，觉得像躺在妈妈的怀里。

就在这时候，咕咚咕咚的声音更清楚了，还带着暖暖的热气，像温牛奶冒出的白烟。暖暖睁开眼，看见光晕里有一条小小的通道，通向一个更亮的地方。她好奇地探头一看——哇！里面有一颗小小的、跳动的光点，一闪一闪，像一颗种子在发芽。

暖暖轻轻问：你是谁呀？光点不会说话，只是更温柔地跳着，像在说：我在等一个小宝宝呀。暖暖忽然觉得心里软软的，她明白啦——这颗小小的光点，就是妈妈的心跳呀！

暖暖轻轻抱住那颗光点，小声说：小宝宝，你听到了吗？妈妈的心跳像小雨滴，咕咚咕咚，陪着你长大。外面的世界有软软的风、圆圆的月亮，还有好多好多的颜色，等你准备好了，它们都会来迎接你。

暖暖说完，光点闪了闪，像在微笑。暖暖也笑了，她把自己变成一颗小小的星星灯笼，挂在光晕边上，说：我陪着你呀。然后，她轻轻哼起一首歌，那歌声像摇篮曲一样，跟着咕咚咕咚的心跳，飘呀飘，飘进那个温暖的世界。

小宝宝，你听到了吗？那是妈妈的声音，也是暖暖的声音。世界很大，但爱就像心跳，一直陪着你。等你准备好了，睁开眼睛，就能看见满天的小星星，都在对你眨眼睛呢！

✨ 故事小语
世界充满了温柔的惊喜，等待我们去发现。就像小星星暖暖那样，带着好奇心和爱，你会发现每一处都藏着光。

The Whale's Lullaby (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

Tweet, tweet, sleep now, little one. Let the moon's silver thread pull you close, like a cradle of clouds. Tonight, Mama Bird tells a story of the sea, where the biggest heart in the ocean sings a gentle song.

Far away, under the waves, lived a great blue whale named Balu. Balu was so big, his back was like a soft island, and his song was so deep, it made the water shiver with kindness. But Balu was worried, for his ocean home was getting colder, and the ice was melting away.

One quiet evening, as Balu swam near the surface, he saw a tiny bird floating on a leaf. It was Chirp, the little bird from the eaves, who had flown far to find the sea. 「Oh, little one,」 sang Balu, 「the water is losing its chill, and my friends the polar bears have less ice to rest upon.」

Chirp tilted his head and tweeted, 「What can I do? I am so small.」 Balu's tail made a gentle splash, and he sang, 「Even a small voice can make a big sound. When you sing, the wind listens. When you whisper, the waves carry it.」

So Chirp fluffed his feathers and sang a tiny song, a song about green grass and cool rivers, about children planting trees and people loving the earth. Balu joined in, and their voices wove together, rising up to the clouds.

The moon smiled down, and the stars twinkled like little lanterns. The song floated over the ice, and the ice felt a little less lonely. The song drifted to the shores, and the sand remembered to be soft. The song reached the city, and the walls began to grow green with vines.

「Little one,」 Balu hummed, 「do you feel the heartbeat of the earth? It goes boom-boom, like a drum, just like your mama's heart when you are nestled close.」 Chirp felt a warm glow, like the golden edge of dawn, and he knew that love could wrap the world.

The whale's lullaby carried on, a gentle rhythm of waves and wind, of 「shh」 and 「whoosh,」 of 「tweet」 and 「hum.」 It sang of a home where ice and ocean, bird and whale, all lived together in harmony.

And as the moon rose high, Balu sang to the unborn child, 「Little one, can you hear? The earth is your home, and it is so very dear. When you are ready, the soft wind and round moon will be waiting, and the lullaby will always be near.」

Tweet, tweet, sleep now, little one. Feel the gentle wrap of the water, the warmth of Mama's heart. The world is safe and full of love, and you will grow strong and kind, just like the whale's lullaby, soft and deep, forever in your soul.

✨ Story Lesson
Even the smallest voice can help protect our big, beautiful home, and love is a song that wraps us all.

月亮摇篮曲（胎教故事）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

夜深了，月光像软软的棉被，轻轻铺在海面上。小海螺小旋旋从海草森林的家里探出头，想看看月亮是不是真的像奶奶说的那样，会唱摇篮曲。

小旋旋说：原来是这样呀……月亮真的亮亮的，像一颗大大的珍珠。它慢慢地升起来，把银色的光洒在每一朵浪花上。

小旋旋轻轻游向海面，身边的泡泡咕嘟咕嘟地往上冒，好像一串串小灯笼，照亮它前面的路。海水暖暖的，轻轻托着小旋旋的壳，像妈妈的手掌包裹着它。

这时，一阵温柔的声音从水面上飘下来——嗯~~~啊~~~嗯~~~，像妈妈在哼着歌。小旋旋竖起耳朵听，那声音轻轻的，软软的，像海草在跳舞，又像小鱼在吐泡泡。

小旋旋问：月亮月亮，是你在唱歌吗？月亮没有回答，只是把光变得更柔和，像一层薄薄的纱，盖在小旋旋身上。

小旋旋继续往前游，游过一片会发光的珊瑚丛。珊瑚们披着月光，闪着淡蓝和粉色的光，像在眨眼睛。小旋旋觉得心里暖暖的，好像有什么东西在轻轻跳动——咚、咚、咚，像小鼓在敲。

那是什么呢？小旋旋仔细听，原来是自己的心跳呀，还有别的声音混在一起。它听到了很远很远的地方，有一个熟悉的声音在轻轻说：小宝宝，你听到了吗？我在等你呢。

小旋旋想：原来妈妈的心跳声，和月亮的光一样温柔呀。它感觉水流轻轻环绕着自己，像被一个巨大的怀抱拥着，不冷也不黑，只有安全和柔软。

小旋旋闭上眼，听那摇篮曲越来越近。它觉得自己像一片羽毛，在月光里飘呀飘，飘过海草森林，飘过珊瑚丛，飘到妈妈的心跳声旁边。

小旋旋轻轻说：小宝宝，你听到了吗？那是妈妈的声音呀。她一直在唱歌给你听，等你准备好，外面的世界有软软的风和圆圆的月亮等着你。现在，就像我这样，闭上眼睛，让月光陪着你，安心地长大吧。

✨ 故事小语
妈妈的爱像月光一样，一直温柔地包围着你，等待着你慢慢长大。

The Upside-Down Fu (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

Little Star, high in the night sky, watched the house below glow warm and golden. Tonight, Mama was placing a special red sign on the door—a sign with the word 'Fu' written in shining ink.

'Upside down, little one,' Mama whispered, her voice soft as a lullaby. 'We put the Fu upside down so that good fortune will tumble right into our home.'

Little Star blinked, and a tiny giggle floated down from the sky. Upside down? What a funny, wonderful idea!

Mama's hand rested on her round, warm belly, right where you were sleeping. 'Can you hear my heart, little one? Thump-thump, thump-thump, like a gentle drum.'

And you, deep in your cozy sea, kicked a tiny foot—right against her palm. It was your own little way of saying, 'I hear you, Mama.'

Then Mama began to hum a quiet song, a song about the moon and the stars, about the red sign and the good fortune it would bring. The hum wrapped around you like the softest cloud, a cloud that swayed gently, back and forth, back and forth.

Outside, the wind rustled the bamboo leaves, whispering, 'Fu, fu, fu.' The lanterns on the porch swayed, painting little shadows that danced on the walls. Everything was safe and still.

Little Star leaned closer, and a warm glow filled the room—not from the lantern, but from the light in Mama's eyes. 'Little one,' she said, 'when you are ready, you will see this red sign, and you will know: good fortune is always tumbling toward you, just like the Fu.'

She placed her other hand over her heart, and the thump-thump became a soft, steady rhythm. 'Sleep safe, little one, Mama is here. The moon is round, the wind is kind, and the Fu is upside down—just for you.'

And so, under the starlight, with the heartbeat drumming softly, you dreamed of red signs tumbling like leaves, and the world waited, warm and golden, for you to arrive.

✨ Story Lesson
Love and hope are always waiting, even when things are turned upside down.

2026年8月8日 · 星期六
小水滴的星光之旅（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

在很高很高的天上，有一朵软软的云朵妈妈。她的怀里，躺着一颗亮晶晶的小水滴，小小的，圆圆的，像一颗透明的珍珠。云朵妈妈轻轻摇着它，哼着听不清词的歌谣：呼……噜……呼……噜……小水滴跟着摇啊摇，暖洋洋的。

有一天，小水滴听到一个声音，远远的，像从大地深处传来：咚……咚……咚……那声音轻轻的，却好温暖。小水滴问：云朵妈妈，那是什么声音呀？云朵妈妈温柔地说：那是大地妈妈的心跳声，她在等你呢。小水滴眨眨眼睛，心里痒痒的，像有一片小羽毛在挠。

云朵妈妈用风儿轻轻托起小水滴，说：去吧，去看看下面的世界。小水滴飘啊飘，穿过一片金灿灿的阳光。阳光像暖暖的蜂蜜，裹着它，甜丝丝的。小水滴觉得身上暖烘烘的，好像被一双大手轻轻捧着。

它飘过一片蓝色的湖，湖水像一面大镜子，映着天空的白云。湖里的小鱼吐着泡泡，咕嘟咕嘟，泡泡升上来，碰到小水滴，变成一串亮晶晶的小铃铛，叮当叮当响。小水滴笑了，笑得浑身颤悠悠的。

它继续往下飘，遇见一片绿油油的草地。草叶上开着一朵小黄花，花瓣上有一滴露珠，跟小水滴一样亮晶晶。露珠说：嗨，小水滴，你要去哪里呀？小水滴说：我要去找那个心跳声。露珠说：那一定是很美的地方，祝你一路顺风。小水滴点点头，心里暖暖的。

小水滴飘过一片花园，花儿们正弯着腰，把香味儿一点一点送进风里。玫瑰香香的，茉莉甜甜的，还有一缕淡淡的桂花香，像是妈妈手上的味道。小水滴深深地吸了一口气，觉得整个世界都香喷喷的。

忽然，小水滴听到那个心跳声更近了：咚……咚……咚……它低头一看，下面有一片温柔的光，像月亮洒在水面上的银光，又像星星落在草丛里的微光。那光暖暖的，软软的，像妈妈的怀抱。小水滴轻轻落进光里，一下子被包裹住了，像泡在温温的牛奶里，又像躲在厚厚的棉被里。

小水滴听到一个声音，像是从很远很远的地方传来，又像是就在耳边：小宝贝，你听到了吗？那是妈妈的心跳声。小水滴觉得好安心，它闭上眼睛，跟着心跳声一摇一摇，像小时候在摇篮里。

小水滴想：我不用急着长大，我可以慢慢地，慢慢地，等自己准备好了，再去看更大的世界。反正，妈妈的心跳声一直会陪着我，像一盏小灯，亮亮的，暖暖的。

小宝宝，你听到了吗？那个心跳声，也是你的。你安心地长大吧，等你准备好了，外面的世界有软软的风，有圆圆的月亮，还有好多好多温柔的声音。现在，你就在妈妈的怀里，听着心跳声，像小水滴一样，睡吧，睡吧……

✨ 故事小语
每一次等待都充满了爱，就像小水滴在云朵里慢慢长大。宝宝，你也在妈妈的爱里安心成长，世界正温柔地等着你。

The Cloud Balloon's Lullaby (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

High above the sleepy hills, a little balloon named Puff floated on a silver string. It hummed a tiny tune, puff-puff, puff-puff, waiting for a gentle wind to carry it to the clouds.

Puff was not a big balloon, nor a fast one, but it was soft and round, and it loved to drift. 'Puff-puff, puff-puff, I go so slow, to see the clouds and watch them glow.'

A little bird perched on a nearby branch, tilting its head. 'Where are you going, Puff?' it chirped. Puff swayed gently: 'To the cloud sea, where the white waves are soft as feathers, and the sun dips its toes in the golden light.'

'May I come?' asked the bird. 'Of course,' said Puff, 'hold on to my string, and we'll float together.' So the bird held on, and they rose, slow and steady, like a leaf on a gentle river.

Up, up, up they went, past the treetops that whispered shh-shh, past the sleepy cows that mooed a soft good-day. The wind carried them like a mother's hand, warm and safe.

Soon they reached the cloud sea. Oh, what a sight! The clouds rolled like gentle waves, soft and white, with little pockets of pink and gold. Puff dipped its nose into a cloud, and it felt like cool cotton candy, melting on the tongue.

They floated over a rainbow that arched like a bridge, and under it, a herd of cloud sheep were grazing on marshmallow grass. 'Baa-baa,' they sang, 'the sky is sweet, and the sun is kind.'

Puff and the bird drifted on, and the clouds parted to reveal a warm, golden glow. It was the sun, sinking low, painting the sky in peach and lavender. 'Oh,' whispered the bird, 'it's like a dream.'

Puff felt a gentle hum from below, a soft thump-thump, thump-thump, like a heartbeat in the sky. It was the sound of all the mothers in the world, humming to their little ones. Puff closed its eyes and listened.

'Little one, can you hear?' Puff sang softly. 'The heart that beats is a drum of love, wrapped around you like a warm glove. Thump-thump, thump-thump, you are safe, you are snug, like a chick in an egg, like a bud in a hug.'

The bird snuggled closer to Puff, and together they floated in the golden light. 'When you are ready,' Puff whispered, 'the soft wind and the round moon will be waiting. But for now, just grow safe and sound, like a star in the night, like a pearl in the deep.'

The sun dipped lower, and Puff and the bird began their gentle descent, back to the sleepy hills. They floated down, down, down, past the whispering trees, past the mooing cows, until they landed softly on the grass, right beside a little cottage.

Through the window, they could see a mother resting, her hand on her belly, humming the same tune. Puff smiled. 'That's the song,' it said. 'The one that never ends, the one that says, I love you, little one, through and through.'

The bird hopped onto Puff's string, and they watched the last of the sun's rays paint the sky in a gentle pink. 'Good morning, little one,' Puff said softly. 'The world is sparkly today, and it's all waiting for you.'

And so Puff and the bird settled into the soft grass, watching the stars begin to twinkle, and they hummed together, puff-puff, chirp-chirp, a lullaby for the little one, safe and warm, growing just fine.

✨ Story Lesson
The world is full of soft wonders, and love is the calm breeze that carries us gently through every journey.

云朵上的棉花糖（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

云朵妈妈轻轻晃着，像摇篮一样。小云朵宝宝在云朵口袋里，听见风儿哼着歌，咕噜咕噜地滚来滚去。

云朵妈妈把口袋裹得软软的，像云朵棉被。她轻轻哼着：'云朵摇啊摇，宝宝睡得好。风儿轻轻吹，月亮微微笑。'

小云朵宝宝觉得痒痒的，咯咯地笑了。他伸出小手手，想摸摸风儿的脸。风儿调皮地吹了一口气，把一朵小云朵吹成了棉花糖的形状，圆圆胖胖的，像一颗软软的糖。

云朵妈妈笑着说：'那是云朵棉花糖，甜丝丝的，等你长大了，就能尝一尝。'小云朵宝宝眨眨眼，看着那团白白的云朵，心里暖暖的。

忽然，云朵妈妈带着小云朵宝宝飘过一片温暖的水域。水光粼粼的，像星星在跳舞。小云朵宝宝听见'扑通���通'的声音，轻轻地问：'妈妈，这是什么声音呀？'

云朵妈妈温柔地说：'那是妈妈的心跳，像小鼓一样，一直陪着你呢。'小云朵宝宝把耳朵贴在云朵上，听见那有节奏的声音，像摇篮曲一样，让他安心极了。

他们继续飘啊飘，经过一片光晕，金灿灿的，像蜂蜜一样甜。小云朵宝宝觉得全身都暖洋洋的，像被太阳抱着。

云朵妈妈轻声说：'小宝宝，你听到了吗？外面的世界有软软的风，有圆圆的月亮，还有妈妈的心跳声。等你准备好了，就可以安心地来。妈妈会一直在这里，等着你。'

小云朵宝宝打了个哈欠，在云朵口袋里蜷成小小一团。他听着妈妈的心跳，像数着星星一样，慢慢地、慢慢地睡着了。

云朵妈妈轻轻哼着歌，飘过蓝蓝的天。阳光暖暖地照着，云朵软软地托着，小云朵宝宝在梦里，闻到了棉花糖的甜味。

✨ 故事小语
妈妈的爱像云朵一样软软的、暖暖的，一直在身边守护着。安心长大吧，世界会温柔地等着你。

The Symmetrical Little Butterfly (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

In a garden where petals nodded slow, a little butterfly woke from her cocoon and saw her reflection in a dewdrop. Her left wing was a perfect mirror of her right, and she whispered, 'I am right here with you.'

She fluttered to a sunflower tall and bright, its golden face turning toward the light. The sunflower said, 'Hello, small friend! My spiral seeds go round and round, a pattern that is safe and sound.'

The butterfly looked at the seeds so neat, each one a tiny star in a spiral street. She counted one, two, three, four, five—and then she began to feel alive.

A gentle breeze began to blow, and it whispered, 'Little one, come and see where the river flows.' It lifted her on a cushion of air, and she drifted along without a care.

She passed a pond where a frog sat still, his voice a low and peaceful trill. 'Ribbit, ribbit,' sang the frog, 'your wings are like a little log—no, no, they're like a lovely page, with patterns from a golden age.'

The butterfly giggled soft and low, 'I think I'll make my wings a show. They're not a log, but they are fine, with spots that sparkle and a gentle line.'

She looked at her left wing, then her right, and saw they were twins in the pale moonlight. 'Oh, symmetry so fair and true, I see myself in every hue.'

A singing pebble by the stream made a tinkle-tinkle, like a dream. 'Plip, plop, plip, plop,' it chimed, 'your wings are like a bell that chimes.'

The butterfly felt a warmth inside, a glow that started where her wings did ride. She thought of a heartbeat, soft and low, a rhythm that she'd always know.

And then she heard it, clear and true, a heartbeat that was meant for you. It came from a window, warm and bright, where a mother hummed in the soft twilight.

The butterfly flew to the windowsill, where a little plant stood quiet and still. A tiny sprout poked through the soil, 'Grow, grow,' it said, 'it's worth the toil.'

And through the glass, the butterfly saw a mother's hand upon her heart, and a little round belly where a baby slept, in a cozy, safe, and secret part.

The butterfly whispered, 'Little one, can you hear? The breeze is soft, the night is near. The sunflower's seeds go round and round, and your own heart will make a sound.'

She felt the heartbeat through the pane, a steady thrum like summer rain. 'That's your mother's heart,' she said, 'a drum that rocks your sleepy head.'

The butterfly wrapped herself in a leaf, a soft green blanket, not too brief. She closed her eyes and hummed a tune, 'Twinkle, twinkle, little moon.'

And then she whispered to the sprout, 'You'll grow up tall and safe, no doubt. And you, little one, will grow too, just like the butterfly, so bright and new.'

When you are ready, the soft wind and the round moon will be waiting. The sunflower will nod, the pebble will sing, and your mother's heart will be your everything.

✨ Story Lesson
Symmetry and balance are around us in nature, and just as the butterfly grows in her own time, you too are growing safe and sound.

月光小舟（胎教期故事）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

在月亮湾的湖面上，泊着一只小小的月光小舟。它不用桨，也不用帆，只靠风儿轻轻一推，就能在银色的水波上滑出好远好远。

月亮湾的湖水啊，清亮亮的，像一块透明的果冻。每到夜晚，星星们就会扑通扑通跳进水里洗澡，把小肚子洗得亮晶晶的，再爬到月光小舟上打盹儿。

可是今天，月光小舟等呀等，星星们却一颗也没来。它有点想问问风儿：小星星们去哪儿啦？风儿悄悄说：它们在云朵妈妈怀里，正做着香香的梦呢。

月光小舟轻轻晃了晃，就像摇篮一样，把水波摇出一圈一圈温柔的笑。它想：等星星们睡醒了，一定会来。那我就安安静静地等吧。

这时，一滴露珠从月牙尖上滑下来，滴答一声，落进了月光小舟的船心。露珠里裹着一束细细的星光，那是星星们托它捎来的问候。

月光小舟把露珠捧在中央，就像捧着一个暖暖的秘密。它忽然觉得，等待的时光也变得温温的、软软的，像盖着一层云朵被。

水波荡啊荡，月光洒啊洒。月光小舟忽然感觉到，在湖底很深很深的地方，有一颗心跳在扑通、扑通地轻轻跳着——那是月亮湾的心跳，也是妈妈的心跳，一下一下，像在哼一支摇篮曲。

小宝宝，你听到了吗？那扑通、扑通的声音，就像月光小舟在轻轻摇着你。你呀，安心地在温暖的港湾里慢慢长大，等你准备好了，月亮湾的风会为你托起小舟，圆圆的月亮会在前方等着你。

月光小舟依然在湖面上轻轻摇晃，它知道，等待不是空空的，而是装满了爱和期待。晚安，小宝宝，月亮湾的水波会一直温柔地守护着你。

✨ 故事小语
等待是温柔的，爱会像月光一样，轻轻包裹着你。

「小小画家的星星调色盘」（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

傍晚，小星星·暖暖提着小小的灯笼，慢慢飘到彩色蜡笔山坡。山坡上的草尖沾着夕阳剩下的橘色，风一吹，像有人在轻轻涂颜色。暖暖低头，忽然看见泥地里躺着一个巴掌大的小调色盘，里面盛着金、粉、蓝、绿四种温柔的颜料。

暖暖用手指蘸了一点金色，在夜空的画布上轻轻一抹——星星们亮了起来，像撒了一把会发光的小糖。她又蘸了粉色，把云朵涂成软软的棉花糖；蘸了蓝色，把远处的小河染成安静的梦。山坡上的小花们都仰起脸，等着属于自己的颜色。

这时，一阵细细的「咚、咚、咚」从很远的地方传来，像画布背面有人在轻轻敲。暖暖把耳朵贴在夜空上，听见那是妈妈的心跳——平稳、温暖，一下一下，像在给这幅画打拍子。原来，所有的颜色加起来，都比不上这一声心跳让人安心。

暖暖忽然明白了：妈妈的心跳，就是夜里最暖的那一笔颜色。它不在调色盘里，却把整个世界都涂得软软的、香香的。她把剩下的绿色轻轻点在自己的小灯笼上，让光也带一点草地的味道。

宝宝，你也在妈妈肚子里，听着这一声「咚、咚、咚」慢慢长大呀。等你会画画的那一天，记得先给妈妈画一颗小小的、暖暖的心。晚安，小画家；晚安，小宝宝。

✨ 故事小语
每一种颜色都是一种温柔；而妈妈的心跳，是夜里最暖的那一笔。

The Little Bird Who Learned a Lullaby (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

High on a soft cloud-meadow, a small bird named Jiujiu practiced his songs. He could tweet bright and loud, but tonight he wanted to sing something softer — a song to help a sleepy little one far below. He flapped to the old Wind and asked, "Wind, how do I sing a lullaby?"

The Wind smiled and spun slowly around him. "Listen," the Wind whispered. "The trees whisper, the brook babbles, the stars hum their quiet tune. Mix a little of each, and you have music." Jiujiu listened: the leaves went sh-sh-sh, the brook went bub-bub, the stars went mmm-mmm. He tried, and a gentle melody floated into the night.

Then, from deep inside the sleeping world, came a sound so soft he almost missed it: a steady "lum... lum... lum." Jiujiu pressed his feathery cheek to the cloud and heard it clearly — a mother's heartbeat, calm and warm, keeping time beneath everything. It was the quiet drum that all lullabies leaned on.

Jiujiu understood. No tweet, no whisper, no hum was as gentle as that heartbeat. So he shaped his song around it, letting the "lum... lum... lum" be the calm center, and his own soft "jiu... jiu..." float like a blanket on top. The little one below wriggled happily, lulled by both.

And you, small sleeper in your mama's warm tide, are already hearing that same drum. One day Jiujiu will sing for you too. Good night, little bird; good night, small one.

✨ Story Lesson
The gentlest sound in the world is a mother's heartbeat, keeping time for every lullaby.

「云朵厨房的甜味信封」（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

云朵精灵·朵朵推开暖暖厨房的窗，把一小团白云放进小烤箱。她撒进月光粉、星星糖，还有一勺「等一等就会甜」的耐心。烤箱呼呼地唱起歌，热气像软软的围巾，绕着朵朵转了一圈又一圈。

不一会儿，一块小小的云朵饼干烤好了，浑身透着暖黄色的光，香香的味道顺着窗缝飘了出去，飘过海草森林，飘过月光海面，一直飘到很远很远的地方。朵朵把饼干装进一个画着小太阳的信封，贴上「给正在长大的你」的标签。

忽然，信封轻轻跳了一下——里面传出了「咚、咚、咚」的声音。朵朵把耳朵贴上去，笑了：那是妈妈的心跳呀，原来这甜甜的味道，一直都连着妈妈肚子里那炉小小的火。每一口香气，都是妈妈在轻轻说「我在呢」。

朵朵明白了：厨房里暖暖的味道，不是凭空来的，是爱一点一点烤出来的；而妈妈的心跳，就是那炉火轻轻的呼吸，不急不慢，永远都在。她把信封轻轻放在宝宝的梦里，让香味陪着他慢慢长大。

宝宝，等你来到这个世界，闻到厨房里甜甜的香气时，就想一想今天这块云朵小饼干吧。晚安，朵朵；晚安，小宝宝。

✨ 故事小语
厨房里甜甜的味道，是爱慢慢烤出来的；妈妈的心跳，就是那炉火轻轻的呼吸。

The Sleepy Rabbit and the Moonlight Letter (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

In Mama's quiet garden, Little Bear An'an was fluffing his soft pillow when a tiny rabbit peeped from behind the roses. The rabbit's ears trembled. "I can't sleep," she whispered. "The night feels too big." An'an patted the ground beside him. "Sit with me. We'll read the moonlight letter together."

The moon had spilled silver light across a broad leaf, and on it were gentle shapes: a hill, a stream, a small curled-up cub. "That's you, learning the world," An'an said. The rabbit traced the shapes with a shy paw. "And that," An'an pointed, "is the whole garden, keeping us warm." The big night suddenly felt like a cozy room.

From somewhere deep and close came a calm "thump-thump, thump-thump." The rabbit stilled. "What is that?" she breathed. An'an smiled. "That is a mother's heartbeat — the safest drum in the world. It says: you are held, even when the night is big." The rabbit laid a paw on her own chest and felt her own small heartbeat answer it.

They stayed like that, two small friends under one moon, the letter of light unfolded between them. The rabbit's ears stopped trembling. "I think I can sleep now," she yawned. An'an tucked the leaf like a blanket over them both.

And you, little one growing in your mama's tide, have that same drum beneath you. When the world feels big, remember: a friend's warm paw, and a mother's steady heartbeat, are two ways the world says you are safe. Good night, small friends; good night, small one.

✨ Story Lesson
A friend's warm paw, and a mother's steady heartbeat, are two ways the world says "you are safe."

「小雨滴的四季旅行」（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

露珠邮递员·露露接到了一封信，要送给「正在长大的小宝宝」。她跳上一滴亮晶晶的小雨，从绵绵小雨花园出发了。第一站是春天：小雨落在嫩芽上，芽芽伸了个懒腰，说「再等等，就快了」。

小雨飘呀飘，到了夏天。荷叶撑起绿伞，青蛙打起小鼓，热乎乎的风里全是青草的味道。露露把信贴在胸口，怕它被太阳晒皱。又飘到秋天：果子红了，叶子金了，风变得轻轻的、凉凉的，像一条软毯子裹住大地。

最后是冬天。小雨变成了小雪花，安安静静地落。世界白白的、暖暖的（因为雪把声音都抱住了）。露露有点累，把耳朵贴在小雪花上——却听见「咚、咚、咚」，一下一下，从很远很近的地方传来。

那是妈妈的心跳呀。原来不管春天、夏天、秋天还是冬天，这声音一直都在，像雨的节奏，不快不慢，陪着肚里的小宝宝慢慢长大。露露忽然不累了：信不用急着送，因为收信的人，正被这声音稳稳地抱着。

宝宝，四季会一轮一轮地转，你也会一天一天地长。可妈妈的心跳，永远是你最安稳的鼓声。晚安，露露；晚安，小宝宝。

✨ 故事小语
四季会轮流转，可妈妈的心跳一直都在，像雨的节奏，陪着你慢慢长大。

「白海豚台风的小船」（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

台风白海豚要来啦！它从很远很远的大海那边，呼呼地游了过来。风是它的尾巴，哗啦哗啦的雨，是它溅起的水花。

爸爸开着小车出门，车轮下面咕嘟咕嘟冒着小水花，小车好像变成了一艘圆圆的小船，在白海豚带来的水世界里，轻轻摇啊摇。

红绿灯的倒影在水洼里晃呀晃，像一串温柔的星星。小船慢慢划过街道，遇见的小树、小房子都朝它轻轻点头。白海豚在天上远远地望着，好像在说：慢慢开，不要急。

不一会儿，小船稳稳停在家的门口。妈妈张开温暖的怀抱，把你——还在肚子里的小宝宝——轻轻护住。你听见了吗？咚咚、咚咚，那是妈妈的心跳，像小船靠岸时，轻轻拍着水面的声音。

台风白海豚还在天上游呀游，可我们的小家，永远是干干爽爽、暖暖和和的小港湾。晚安，我的小船长。

✨ 故事小语
无论外面的风有多大、雨有多急，爸爸妈妈的爱和家，永远是你温暖又安全的小港湾。

The Little Pot's Good-Night Glow (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

In a cozy corner of the kitchen, a tiny potted plant sat on the windowsill, its leaves swaying soft as a lullaby. Every night, when the moon rose round and low, the little pot would whisper to the baby in Mama's belly, 'Float, float, home at last.'

The little pot had a secret wish: to give the baby a good-night glow, a warm little light to say 'I'm here, growing with you.' So it stretched its leaves toward the moon, catching silver beams and dewy drops, and began to hum a tiny tune—hum-hum, hum-hum, like a sleepy bee.

A breeze blew through the window, soft and slow, and carried the little pot's song up, up, into the night. The song swirled around the room, wrapping Mama's belly in a gentle bubble of warmth, like a cloud cradling a star. Can you feel it, little one? The soft cloud walls, the cozy sway—the world is holding you close.

The little pot's leaves turned a soft, golden green, glowing like a tiny lantern. It painted the room with a gentle light, warm as honey, soft as fur. The moonlight outside peeked in, and the stars winked, and the pot's glow grew brighter, warmer, like a blanket of light wrapping you tight.

Then, through the glow, the little pot heard a sound—thump, thump, thump. It was Mama's heartbeat, steady and strong, like a drum calling you home. The little pot smiled and whispered, 'Little one, can you hear it? That's Mama's heart, singing you a lullaby of love.'

The glow wrapped around the heartbeat, and the heartbeat wrapped around you, and the little pot's leaves swayed, swayed, swayed. The room was filled with the smell of fresh earth and the taste of sweet dreams, and the pot's light was a cradle of stars, rocking you gently.

And the little pot said, 'Float, float, home at last. When you are ready, the soft wind and round moon will be waiting, and Mama's heart will be your pillow, warm and true. Good night, little one, the moon is with you.'

✨ Story Lesson
Even the smallest, quietest things hold a world of love, and waiting for you is the gentlest song of all.

月光摇篮曲（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

啾啾，睡吧。屋檐下的小鸟啾啾，望着圆圆的月亮，想为肚子里的小宝宝唱一首歌。它轻轻抖了抖羽毛，发出沙沙的响声，像风儿在树叶间散步。

啾啾想：小宝宝在妈妈的肚子里，一定像躺在云朵做的摇篮里，轻轻摇啊摇。

于是，啾啾展开翅膀，飞过亮晶晶的星星，来到月光海面。海浪轻轻地拍着，哗啦哗啦，像妈妈的心跳，咚咚，咚咚。啾啾把月光装进羽毛里，羽毛变得软软的，暖暖的。

它继续飞，飞过云端棉田，那里有白白的云朵，像棉花糖一样。啾啾用喙轻轻啄了一小片，云朵发出噗噗的声音，像枕头在呼吸。

这时，一阵温柔的哼歌声从远处传来，像云朵被子一样，把啾啾轻轻裹住。哼歌里藏着摇篮曲的节拍，摇啊摇，摇啊摇。啾啾闭上眼睛，感觉自己也变成了小宝宝，被妈妈抱在怀里。

小宝宝，你听到了吗？那是妈妈的哼歌声，像温暖的毛线团，一圈一圈，把你包在中间。你感觉到吗？月光像星星灯笼，照亮你小小的梦。

啾啾带着羽毛和云朵，飞回屋檐下，它把月光和哼歌声，编成一首摇篮曲，藏在窝里。等你准备好了，外面的世界有软软的风和圆圆的月亮，等你。

晚安，小宝宝，月亮陪着你，妈妈的心跳陪你。啾啾，睡吧。

✨ 故事小语
爱与守护，就像月光一样温柔地包裹着每一个小生命，等待他安心长大。

The Little Cloud Whale's Lullaby (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

Little Bear An'an floated on a soft white cloud, his fur puffing in the breeze. A tiny cloud whale swam near, humming a gentle tune that sparkled like stardust.

Hush, hush, the wind whispered, and the cloud whale's hum grew warm like a blanket. An'an snuggled into the cloud, feeling the rhythm of the song swaying him to and fro.

The whale's song carried a wish: to help the earth breathe easier, to cool the melting ice and plant new green. An'an listened closely, his heart beating slow and steady like a drum.

Whoosh, the wind blew, and the cloud whale's song turned into a stream of silver notes that floated down to the earth. They landed on a bare hill and became tiny seeds, waiting for rain.

Pitter-patter, the rain came, and the seeds began to sprout. An'an watched as little green shoots reached for the sky, their leaves dancing in the breeze.

The cloud whale sang, and the shoots grew into trees, their branches stretching wide like open arms. An'an felt a warm glow, as if the trees were hugging him from below.

He hummed along, and his voice joined the whale's, carrying the wish even farther. The trees swayed, their leaves making soft shushing sounds, like a lullaby for the earth.

Little one, can you hear it? That gentle humming wraps you like a cloud blanket, carrying the rhythm of a lullaby. It is the song of the trees, the wind, and the whale, all singing for you.

An'an closed his eyes, feeling the warmth of the cloud and the song. He knew that when you are ready, the soft wind and round moon will be waiting, and the earth will welcome you with open arms.

And so, floating in the sky, An'an drifted off to sleep, the cloud whale's hum fading into a peaceful hush. The stars twinkled, and the earth below grew calm, all wrapped in a cradle of stars.

✨ Story Lesson
Even the smallest wish can travel far on a song, and home is always waiting in the warmth of love.

2026年8月12日 · 星期三
雨滴宝宝的彩虹滑梯（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

在云朵妈妈的怀里，有一颗亮晶晶的小雨滴，名字叫叮咚。它整天在云朵被子里滚来滚去，听风儿讲故事，看太阳公公把云朵染成橘红色。小宝宝，你也在妈妈肚子里滚来滚去吧？

有一天，叮咚听见远处传来一阵轻轻的声音，像铃铛在唱歌。它竖起小耳朵，听见彩虹姐姐在云朵的尽头喊：『叮咚，来玩滑梯吧！我这里有七种颜色的光，滑起来可暖和啦！』小宝宝，你听见彩虹姐姐的呼喊了吗？

叮咚的心扑通扑通跳，它好想去看看彩虹。可是云朵妈妈的怀抱软软的、暖暖的，叮咚有点舍不得。它小声说：『妈妈，我想去滑彩虹滑梯，可是我会不会掉下去呀？』小宝宝，你也会这样担心吗？

云朵妈妈用软软的手掌轻轻摸摸叮咚：『傻孩子，风会托着你，云会接着你。你只要张开小手臂，就像小鸟学飞一样。』小宝宝，妈妈也这样告诉你：别怕，有妈妈在呢。

叮咚深吸一口气，从云朵妈妈的怀里探出小脑袋。风儿呼呼地吹来，像一双看不见的大手，轻轻托住它。叮咚觉得自己的身体飘了起来，像一片羽毛，又像一只小气球。小宝宝，你也感受到风儿的温柔了吗？

它飘过一片蓝蓝的天空，看见太阳公公笑眯眯地向它点头。它飘过一朵粉粉的云，那朵云像棉花糖一样，发出甜甜的味道。叮咚咯咯地笑，声音像一串小铃铛。小宝宝，你闻到棉花糖的香味了吗？

忽然，叮咚看见一道弯弯的彩虹桥，就架在云朵和大地之间。彩虹姐姐在桥头招手，它的颜色像七种果冻：红红的、橙橙的、黄黄的、绿绿的、蓝蓝的、靛靛的、紫紫的。小宝宝，你数数有几种颜色呀？

叮咚轻轻落在彩虹的最高处，感觉脚下软软的，像踩在云朵上。它闭上眼睛，呼地一下往下滑——风在耳边啦啦啦地唱，光在周围闪呀闪，像有无数小星星在跳舞。小宝宝，你也跟着一起滑吧！

滑到一半，叮咚忽然看见彩虹底下有一圈暖暖的光，像妈妈张开的怀抱。那光柔柔地托住它，让它慢悠悠地飘起来，像躺在软软的小窝里，再也不怕掉下去。小宝宝，那圈光也正托着你呢。

叮咚觉得好安心呀，它听见那光里传来咚咚、咚咚的声音，像妈妈的心跳，又像小船轻轻拍着水。它知道，不管自己飘到哪里，总有一圈光会接住它，总有一个怀抱在等着它。小宝宝，你听见妈妈的心跳了吗？

小宝宝，你听见了吗？那圈暖暖的光，也轻轻托着你呢。等你准备好了，外面的世界有软软的彩虹、甜甜的风，还有好多好多的光在等你。现在，你只要安心地长大就好。妈妈会一直陪着你。

叮咚顺着光，又飘回云朵妈妈的怀里。它把彩虹的颜色藏在心里，闭上眼睛，听着风儿唱起摇篮曲，慢慢睡着了。晚安，小宝宝，月亮会陪着你，妈妈也一直陪着你，一直陪着你。

✨ 故事小语
爱与守护永远都在，宝宝可以安心长大。

The Sweet and Salty Zongzi (Prenatal, 3-5 min)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

In a cozy kitchen, a little bamboo leaf held two tiny zongzi — one sweet, one salty. They swayed gently in the warm steam, waiting to be wrapped in love.

Sway, sway, went the little bamboo leaf, humming a soft rustling song. The sweet zongzi was round and sticky, with a heart of red dates and honey. The salty zongzi was plump and tender, with a heart of mushrooms and savory rice.

They looked at each other, sweet and salty, and giggled. 「You smell like sunshine and honey,」 said the salty one. 「And you smell like the deep, rich earth,」 said the sweet one. They didn't know which was better, but they liked each other just the same.

A gentle breeze, whoosh-whoosh, floated through the window, carrying the scent of fresh bamboo. It wrapped around the two little zongzi like a soft, warm hug. 「Little ones,」 whispered the breeze, 「you are both loved, just as you are.」

Then Mama's hands, warm and gentle, picked up the bamboo leaf and folded it into a little nest. She placed the sweet zongzi and the salty zongzi side by side, and tied them together with a red string, snug and safe.

As the pot bubbled, bloop-bloop-bloop, the two zongzi nestled together, feeling the warmth rise around them. It was like being held in a cozy cradle, swaying in the steam.

Little one, can you feel it? That warm embrace, like a ring of light around you, holding you steady and safe. That's how Mama and Papa hold you now, and how the world will hold you when you are ready.

The sweet zongzi said, 「I'm glad we're together.」 The salty zongzi nodded, 「Me too. Sweet and salty, we make a perfect pair.」

And as the steam rose, the kitchen filled with a gentle glow, like the golden edge of dawn. The two little zongzi drifted into a happy doze, dreaming of the day they would be shared with love.

When you are ready, little one, the soft wind and the round moon will be waiting. But for now, just grow safe and sound, wrapped in the warmth of those who love you.

Good morning, little one. The world is sparkly today.

✨ Story Lesson
Differences are beautiful; love wraps them all together.

小面团暖暖的太阳之旅（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

在暖暖的厨房里，有一个小面团，名字叫软软。它躺在面板上，听着锅碗瓢盆叮叮当当的声音，心里痒痒的，想去看看外面的世界。小宝宝，你也在听吗？那些声音像轻轻的摇篮曲。

软软正想着，一只大手轻轻把它捧起来，揉了揉，又拍了拍。「哎呀呀，真舒服呀！」软软感觉像坐在摇摇车里，晃呀晃，晃呀晃。小宝宝，你也在妈妈肚子里轻轻晃吗？那是爱的摇晃。

大手把软软放进一个小碗里，盖上湿湿的纱布，像盖了一床小被子。「咕嘟咕嘟」，软软听见自己的肚子在唱歌，那是酵母宝宝在跳舞呢！小宝宝，你听见妈妈的心跳了吗？那是为你唱的摇篮曲。

软软在黑暗的小碗里，觉得好安静。它轻轻问：「谁在陪着我呀？」一个细细的声音回答：「是我呀，我是酵母宝宝，我在帮你长大呢！」小宝宝，你身边也有这样的陪伴吗？那是妈妈温柔的目光。

软软感觉身体里热乎乎的，好像有一群小太阳在散步。「咕噜咕噜」，它变得更胖了，更软了，像一朵蓬蓬的云。小宝宝，你也在慢慢长大，像软软一样，变得圆圆的、暖暖的。

不知过了多久，纱布被掀开，阳光像蜂蜜一样浇进来。「哇！」软软看见窗台上有一盆小绿芽，正朝它点头呢。小宝宝，你感觉到了吗？阳光也穿过妈妈的肚皮，轻轻抚摸你。

「你好呀，小面团！」小绿芽说，「你闻起来像甜甜的麦田。」软软吸了吸鼻子，真的闻到了太阳的味道，香香的，暖暖的。小宝宝，那是爱的味道，你闻到了吗？

大手又把软软拿出来，在面板上搓呀搓，揉呀揉，像在给它按摩。「咯咯咯」，软软痒得笑出声来。它被捏成圆圆的，胖胖的，像一个小月亮。小宝宝，你也在妈妈的温柔里打滚吗？

「小宝宝，你听见了吗？」软软对着窗外轻轻说，「外面的世界有软软的风，还有圆圆的月亮，等你准备好了，它们都会来陪你。」你听见了吗？它们都在等你。

软软被送进烤箱，那里像一个温暖的小山洞。「呼噜呼噜」，它听着火苗唱歌，感觉自己被阳光包裹着，越来越暖，越来越香。小宝宝，你也在一个温暖的山洞里，听着妈妈的心跳。

「叮咚！」烤箱门打开了，软软变成了一个金黄的小面包，浑身散发着甜甜的香气。它闭上了眼睛，在暖暖的阳光下，满足地舒了一口气。小宝宝，等你准备好了，你也会遇见你的阳光。

小宝宝，你听到了吗？世界就像一个大大的烤箱，等你准备好了，它就会给你最温柔的拥抱。就像软软，变成金黄的小面包，被爱包围着，等待你的到来。

✨ 故事小语
世界充满温暖与爱，等待你慢慢长大，给你最温柔的拥抱。

萤火虫的小灯笼（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

夏天的夜晚，草丛里亮起一盏小小的灯，忽明忽暗。那不是星星掉下来了，是一只提着小灯笼的萤火虫，名字叫小萤。它飞起来，翅膀扑棱扑棱，像一片会发光的小叶子。

小萤提着自己的灯，在草叶间慢慢地飞。飞过薄荷叶，飞过狗尾巴草，飞过一颗还在睡觉的露珠。草丛沙沙沙地响，露珠被照亮了，像一颗透明的小月亮。

它飞到一扇窗户前，看见屋里亮着暖黄色的光。一位妈妈正坐在床边，轻轻地拍着圆圆的肚子，哼着一支软软的歌。咚咚、咚咚，那是妈妈的心跳，稳稳的，暖暖的。

小萤停在窗台上，把灯笼的光，调成和屋里一样暖的颜色。它听见妈妈在唱：宝宝呀，睡吧，睡吧。那声音像溪水，又像月光，滴答滴答，一下一下落在心尖上。

小宝宝，你听见了吗？小萤的灯笼一闪一闪，是在替你照亮还没走完的路。你呀，安心地在妈妈暖暖的肚子里长大，长呀长。

夜深了，小萤还停在窗台上，一下一下地亮着。它想，等你来到这个世界，第一眼看见的，一定是这样温柔的光。

晚安，小萤。晚安，还在妈妈肚子里做着小梦的小宝宝。

✨ 故事小语
最温柔的光，是愿意为别人点亮的那一盏。你还没来到这个世界，就已经有人在黑夜里为你留灯了。

小闹钟想睡懒觉（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

窗台上住着一只小闹钟，名字叫叮当。每天天一亮，它就叮铃铃、叮铃铃地响，把全家人都叫起来。它觉得自己可神气啦。

可是今天早晨，叮当忽然不想响了。它把铃声含在嘴里，咕噜咕噜的，就是不肯吐出来。它想：让我也赖一会儿床吧，就一小会儿。

妈妈走过来，没有生气，反而轻轻笑了。妈妈说：叮当，你累啦？那就歇一歇，妈妈先给你倒一小杯温水，润润嗓子。

叮当咕噜咕噜喝了几口，嗓子舒服多了。它听见爸爸在院子里沙沙沙地扫叶子，听见小鸟在窗外啾啾啾地唱歌，听见宝宝在妈妈肚子里扑通扑通地翻了个身。

小宝宝，你听见了吗？那是爸爸的笑声，从院子里传进来，像春天的雷一样，让人安心。原来睡醒以后的世界，这么热闹，又这么温柔。

叮当忽然觉得自己有劲儿了。它清清嗓子，叮铃铃、叮铃铃地响起来，比哪一天都响得开心。

宝宝呀，等你想睡懒觉的时候，也可以像叮当一样，先歇一歇，再开开心心地醒过来。早安，小闹钟。早安，小宝宝。

✨ 故事小语
想休息的时候可以休息一下，就像爸爸妈妈累了会歇一歇。可该醒的时候，还是要开开心心地醒过来。

一朵云的小睡（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

天上有一朵小小的云，白白的，软软的，像一床刚晒过的棉被。它从东边飘到西边，飘呀飘，忽然觉得有点困了。

它想找一个地方睡午觉。可是天空那么大，风又一直推着它走。小云打了个哈欠，身子软软地往下沉了一点。

一棵大树的树梢，正好托住了它。树上的知了放轻了声音，吱——地叫了一半就停了。太阳也把光调得柔柔的。

小云趴在树梢上，听着树叶沙沙沙，听着远处溪水叮叮咚咚，像一首没有词的摇篮曲。它一点点闭上眼睛。

小宝宝，你感觉到了吗？这世界就像一双看不见的手，正轻轻托着你，像大树托着小云一样。星星们手拉手，围成一个小小的摇篮，轻轻摇着你。

小云睡了一个甜甜的午觉。醒来的时候，天边有一抹淡淡的金，它觉得自己轻得像一片羽毛，又能飘啦。

宝宝呀，累的时候就歇一歇。等你准备好了，外面的世界有软软的风，有圆圆的月亮，还有好多好多安静又温柔的时光，等着你。

✨ 故事小语
累了的时候，就停下来歇一歇，世界会轻轻接住你。安安静静的时光，也是甜甜的。

窗台上的小水珠（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

下过一场雨啦。窗台上，滚着一颗小小的水珠，圆溜溜、亮晶晶的，像一滴会发光的眼泪。雨还在檐下滴答、滴答地落。

小水珠哪儿也不去。它就安安静静地待着，看云一朵一朵走过，看蚂蚁排着队回家，看一只蜻蜓停下来，用翅膀蘸了蘸它。风从窗户缝里轻轻溜进来，呼——呼——地跟它打招呼。

太阳出来了。小水珠把整个天空都装进肚子里——蓝蓝的天，白白的云，还有一道淡得像梦一样的彩虹。它亮得晃眼睛。

屋里，妈妈哼起歌来。那歌声软软的，甜甜的，像一滴露水落在花瓣上，滴答。小水珠听着听着，觉得自己也要变成一首歌了。

小宝宝，你听见了吗？妈妈在给你哼歌呢。那歌声一圈一圈，像温暖的毛线团，把你轻轻地裹在中间。摇啊摇，摇啊摇。

太阳暖暖地照着。小水珠慢慢地、慢慢地变轻了，飞上了天，变成了一朵很小很小的云。它知道，明天它还会再变成雨，落回这扇窗台。

宝宝呀，你也像一颗小水珠，正安安静静地长大。等时候到了，你就会闪闪发亮，照见属于你的天空。

✨ 故事小语
再小的生命，也有自己的位置。安安静静地待着，也能照见整个天空。

风铃的夏天（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

屋檐下挂着一串风铃，细细的银管，一碰就叮叮当当响。这个夏天，风铃可忙了，它想把每一阵路过的风，都编成一支歌。

早晨的风来啦，轻轻柔柔的，风铃叮——地应了一声，像刚睡醒的问候。中午的风热乎乎的，风铃叮叮咚咚，像在说：慢慢走，别着急。

傍晚的风最温柔，带着晚饭的香味，从窗户缝里钻进来。风铃响得轻轻的，生怕吵醒了正在妈妈肚子里打盹的小宝宝。

一天，风铃问风：你整天东奔西跑，累不累呀？风笑着回答：不累，因为我要把你的歌，送到很远很远的地方去。

小宝宝，你感觉到了吗？风从窗户缝里溜进来，带着风铃的叮当声，像妈妈的手，一下一下，轻轻地拍着你。

夜深了，风停了。风铃安安静静地挂在屋檐下，可它心里还藏着白天的歌。它想，等小宝宝出生了，一定要把这支歌，唱给他听。

宝宝呀，这个夏天有风，有风铃，有叮叮当当的歌。而你，就是这些歌里，最被期待的那一个。

✨ 故事小语
风会来，风也会停，可歌会一直留在心里。你听见的每一声叮当，都是这个夏天在轻轻跟你说话。

会飞的被子（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

妈妈把一床印着小碎花的被子，晒在阳台的晾衣绳上。被子鼓鼓的，被太阳晒得香香的、蓬蓬的，像一朵暖洋洋的大花。风吹过，它哗啦哗啦地轻轻摆。

忽然，一阵大风呼——地吹过来，把小花被刮上了天！被子摇摇晃晃地飞呀飞，飞过了屋顶，飞过了树梢，变成了一条会飞的被子。

它飞到一朵云旁边，云朵软软地说：要不要来我这儿坐坐？被子摇摇头：我还要回家呢。它飞到一群小鸟旁边，小鸟叽叽喳喳地邀它一起飞。被子扑棱扑棱地摆了摆，说：谢谢你们呀。

被子在天上转了一圈又一圈，看到了整座小城，看到了亮晶晶的河，看到了自家那扇开着的窗。它忽然很想念阳台上暖暖的阳光，和妈妈身上的味道。

小宝宝，你感觉到了吗？一床软软的小被子，正轻轻地盖过来，把你裹成一个小小的、暖暖的茧。你听，被子里还藏着妈妈的心跳，咚咚、咚咚。你呀，正被这个家温柔地等着。

风慢慢小了。小花被稳稳地落回了阳台，爸爸踮起脚，把它重新挂好。妈妈笑着把它抱回屋里：被子也想家啦。

宝宝呀，等你出生了，就睡在这床会飞的小被子里。它有太阳的味道，有风讲过的故事，还有爸爸妈妈满满的爱。晚安，会飞的被子。晚安，小宝宝。

✨ 故事小语
家是最柔软的地方，飞得再远，最后都会回到温暖的床上。你呀，正被这个家温柔地等着。

The Firefly's Little Lantern (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

In the soft summer grass, a tiny light glows on and off. It is not a fallen star — it is a little firefly named Finn, carrying a tiny lantern.

Finn flies slowly from leaf to leaf, past the mint, past the tall grass, past a sleeping dewdrop. The dewdrop lights up like a small transparent moon.

He drifts to a window, where warm yellow light glows inside. A mother sits by the bed, patting her round belly, humming a soft, soft song.

Finn rests on the windowsill and turns his lantern to the same warm color. He hears her sing: sleep now, little one, sleep. The sound is like a stream, like moonlight.

Little one, can you hear it? Finn's lantern blinks on and off, lighting the road you have not yet walked. Rest safely, growing warm and slow inside your mama.

The night deepens. Finn stays on the windowsill, glowing gently. He thinks: when you arrive in this world, the first thing you see will be light as soft as this.

Good night, Finn. Good night, little one, dreaming your small dreams inside mama.

✨ Story Lesson
The gentlest light is the one we light for someone else. Before you even arrive, someone is already keeping a light on for you.

The Little Alarm Clock Sleeps In (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

On the windowsill lives a little alarm clock named Dingdong. Every morning, he rings ding-a-ling, ding-a-ling, and wakes the whole house. He feels very proud.

But this morning, Dingdong does not want to ring. He holds the bell in his mouth — gurgle, gurgle — and will not let it out. Just a little more sleep, he thinks, just one tiny bit.

Mama comes over, and instead of being cross, she smiles. She says: Dingdong, are you tired? Then rest a while. Let me pour you a little cup of warm water first.

Dingdong drinks — gurgle, gurgle — and his throat feels better. He hears papa sweeping leaves in the yard, swish-swish. He hears the birds chirping, tweet-tweet. He hears the baby turn over in mama's belly, thump-thump.

Little one, can you hear it? That is papa's laugh drifting in from the yard, low and warm like distant thunder. The waking world is so busy, and so gentle.

Suddenly Dingdong feels full of energy. He clears his throat and rings — ding-a-ling, ding-a-ling — happier than ever before.

Little one, when you want to sleep in, you may rest a little, then wake up happy again. Good morning, Dingdong. Good morning, little one.

✨ Story Lesson
It is okay to rest when you are tired, just like grown-ups pause to catch their breath. And when it is time, you can wake up happy again.

A Little Cloud's Nap (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

High in the sky floats a small white cloud, soft as a quilt fresh from the sun. It drifts from east to west, and after a while, it feels a little sleepy.

It wants to find a place for a nap. But the sky is so wide, and the wind keeps pushing it along. The little cloud yawns and sinks softly down.

A tall tree's treetop catches it. The cicada lowers its voice — zee — and stops halfway. The sun softens its light too.

The little cloud nestles on the treetop, listening to the leaves — rustle, rustle — and the faraway stream — plip-plop. It is a lullaby without words. Its eyes slowly close.

Little one, can you feel it? The world is like a pair of invisible hands, holding you up, the way the tree holds the little cloud. The stars join hands into a tiny cradle and rock you gently.

The little cloud wakes from a sweet nap. A faint gold glows at the sky's edge, and it feels light as a feather, ready to float again.

Little one, rest when you are tired. When you are ready, the soft wind and the round moon will be waiting, along with so many quiet, gentle hours.

✨ Story Lesson
When you are tired, stop and rest — the world will catch you gently. Quiet time is sweet time too.

The Little Waterdrop on the Windowsill (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

After the rain, a tiny waterdrop rolls onto the windowsill — round and clear, like a shining tear of light.

The little waterdrop goes nowhere. It just sits still, watching clouds drift by, watching ants march home, watching a dragonfly pause to dip its wing.

The sun comes out. The little waterdrop holds the whole sky inside itself — blue sky, white clouds, and a rainbow faint as a dream.

Inside, mama begins to hum. The sound is soft and sweet, like a dewdrop falling on a petal. The little waterdrop listens, and feels it might turn into a song too.

Little one, can you hear it? Mama is humming for you. The song wraps around and around, like a warm ball of yarn, tucking you in the middle.

The sun warms the sill. The little waterdrop grows lighter, lighter, and drifts up to become a very small cloud. It knows tomorrow it will fall again, back to this same windowsill.

Little one, you are like a small waterdrop, growing quietly. When the time comes, you will shine, and hold a sky of your own.

✨ Story Lesson
Even the smallest life has its place. Sitting quietly, it can still hold the whole sky.

The Wind Chime's Summer (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

Under the eaves hangs a wind chime, with thin silver pipes that ring at the slightest touch. This summer, the chime is busy — it wants to turn every passing wind into a song.

The morning wind comes, soft and gentle. The chime answers — ding — like a sleepy greeting. The noon wind blows warm, and the chime rings ding-ding-dong, as if saying: go slowly, no hurry.

The evening wind is tenderest, carrying the smell of dinner through the window crack. The chime rings very softly, careful not to wake the little one dozing in mama's belly.

One day the chime asks the wind: you rush about all day — are you not tired? The wind laughs: no, because I carry your song to far, far places.

Little one, can you feel it? The wind slips through the window, carrying the chime's ding-ding, like a mother's hand, patting you, soft and steady.

Night falls, and the wind stops. The chime hangs quiet under the eaves, but it still holds the day's songs in its heart. When you are born, it will sing them for you.

Little one, this summer has wind, and chimes, and ding-ding songs. And of all those songs, you are the one most hoped for.

✨ Story Lesson
The wind comes and goes, but the song stays in your heart. Every ding you hear is summer speaking to you softly.

The Flying Quilt (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

Mama hangs a little quilt printed with tiny flowers on the balcony line. It is puffy and warm, dried sweet by the sun, like a big cozy flower.

Suddenly a big gust — whoosh! — sweeps the quilt into the sky. It wobbles and floats, over the roof, over the treetops, and becomes a flying quilt.

It floats to a cloud, and the cloud says softly: would you like to sit with me? The quilt shakes its head: I must go home. It floats to some birds, who chirp and invite it to fly along.

The quilt circles the sky, seeing the whole little town, the shining river, and its own open window. Suddenly it misses the warm sun on the balcony, and mama's smell.

Little one, can you feel it? A soft quilt is tucking over you, wrapping you into a small, warm cocoon. You are being gently waited for by this home.

The wind slowly calms. The little quilt drifts down and lands back on the balcony. Papa tiptoes and hangs it up again. Mama smiles and carries it in: the quilt missed home too.

Little one, when you are born, you will sleep in this flying quilt. It holds the sun's smell, the stories the wind told, and all of mama and papa's love. Good night, flying quilt. Good night, little one.

✨ Story Lesson
Home is the softest place. No matter how far you fly, in the end you come back to a warm bed — and this home is waiting gently for you.

小狐狸的第一场雪（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

森林深处，住着一只火红的小狐狸。它听老松树说，等天再冷一点，天上会落下来一种白白软软的东西，叫「雪」。小狐狸从来没见过雪，它等啊等，把耳朵竖得尖尖的。

夜里，风呼呼地刮过树梢，小狐狸缩在洞口，把尾巴盖在鼻尖上。它听见远处有小溪汩汩地流，有猫头鹰咕——咕——地叫，可雪还是没有来。

它有点困了，眼皮一下一下地往下掉。就在它快要睡着的时候，天上忽然飘下一片、两片，轻轻的小白点，像谁撒下了一把会飞的羽毛。

小狐狸一下子蹦起来，追着雪花跑。雪花落在它黑黑的鼻尖上，凉丝丝的；落在它火红的尾巴上，一闪就化了。整个森林，都变成了白茫茫、软绵绵的一片。

小宝宝，你感觉到了吗？这世界上有那么多第一次，都像这场雪一样，正安安静静地等着你。圆圆的月亮，像一盏小夜灯，也陪着小狐狸，等着你呢。

小狐狸在雪地里打了个滚，留下了一串小小的脚印。它想：等你来了，一定要带你来看第一场雪，看脚印在雪地上排成一条回家的路。

宝宝呀，慢慢等，慢慢长。这世界最美的风景，都会为耐心等待的你，一场一场地准备好。

✨ 故事小语
最美的风景，总是为耐心等待的人准备。你还没来，这个世界就已经为你留好了许多第一次。

蒲公英的远行（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

草地上，开着一朵蒲公英。它的头顶，是一颗颗白色的小伞，挤在一起，像一团软软的、会发光的云。风一吹，沙沙沙，小伞们就轻轻地晃。

有一天，风来了。风轻轻地说：走吧，孩子们，我带你们去看外面的世界。小伞们沙沙沙地点头，一朵接一朵，呼——地离开了妈妈的花茎。

它们飘呀飘，飞过了金黄的麦田，飞过了亮晶晶的小河，飞过了正在打盹的小山坡。每一把小伞，都像一艘小小的、透明的船，摇啊摇。

天快黑了。有一把小伞，落在一片安静的草地上，轻轻地说：就在这里吧。它钻进软软的泥土里，盖上一床薄薄的土被子，甜甜地睡着了。

小宝宝，你听见了吗？风里传来一阵温柔的哼歌声，像云朵被子一样，轻轻裹着那颗睡着了的小种子。妈妈也会这样，一路陪着你。

春天来的时候，那颗小种子会发芽，会长成新的蒲公英，再开出许多白色的小伞。到那时，它也会让风，把自己的孩子送去远方。

宝宝呀，你也是一颗正在妈妈肚子里安睡的小种子。等时候到了，你也会在爱里，找到属于你的地方，生根，发芽，开出自己的花。

✨ 故事小语
离开是为了更好的相遇。每一颗小种子，都会在风里找到自己生根发芽的地方。

小猪的泡泡浴（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

小猪哼哼最讨厌洗澡啦。一到洗澡时间，它就哼哼唧唧地往床底下钻，说：我不要洗澡，我不要洗澡！

可是这一天，妈妈在浴缸里放了好多好多泡泡。白白软软的泡泡堆得像小山一样高，还飘着香香的、甜甜的橘子味。

哼哼探出半个脑袋，眼睛一下子亮了。它咕噜咕噜地爬进浴缸，泡泡哗啦哗啦地涌上来，把它裹成了一个圆滚滚的泡泡猪。

它用鼻子拱起一团泡泡，噗——地吹了一口气，泡泡飞起来，在浴室里飘呀飘。妈妈在一旁看得咯咯直笑。

小宝宝，你听见了吗？那是爸爸在浴室门口笑呢，声音低低的、暖暖的，像春天的雷，让人听了心里踏踏实实。

洗完澡，哼哼被一条大毛巾裹得严严实实，香喷喷的，软乎乎的。它忽然觉得，洗澡也没那么讨厌嘛。

宝宝呀，等你出生了，我们也会给你洗香香的泡泡浴。你会像哼哼一样，发现原来洗澡，是一件这么好玩、这么温暖的事。

✨ 故事小语
有些事看着不情愿，试过才知道有多好玩。洗澡呀，就是把一天的脏脏，都变成香香的泡泡。

影子的朋友（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

傍晚，太阳斜斜地照进屋子，墙上出现了一个安静的小影子。它跟着光，慢慢地移动，像一条温柔的小河，汩汩地流。

小影子不吵，也不闹。白天，它躲在脚下；傍晚，它爬上墙，变成一只小黑猫，变成一座小山，变成一条会游的小鱼。风从窗缝钻进来，呼——呼——地逗它。

它陪着窗边的绿植，陪着桌上的水杯，陪着妈妈一起叠衣服。妈妈的手一抬，小影子也抬起手；妈妈低下头，小影子也低下头。衣服叠好了，发出沙沙的轻响。

天一点一点暗下来。小影子的颜色慢慢变淡，最后，它轻轻地说：我回家啦，明天傍晚，我还会来陪你。

小宝宝，你感觉到了吗？星星们手拉手，围成一个小小的摇篮，轻轻摇着你，像小影子一样，安静地守着你。

夜里，小影子不见了。可它其实哪儿也没去，它就藏在光的后面，等第二天太阳出来，再轻轻巧巧地爬回墙上。

宝宝呀，这世上有一种陪伴，是安安静静的。它不说话，却一直都在——就像影子，就像家里每一个等你出生的人。

✨ 故事小语
有些陪伴是安静的，不说话，却一直都在。就像影子，就像正在等你出生的家人。

小刺猬的苹果（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

秋天到了，树林里落满了金黄的叶子。小刺猬在树下发现了一个又大又红的苹果，香喷喷的，亮晶晶的。

它想把苹果背回家，可苹果圆溜溜的，它用爪子推，苹果骨碌碌滚走了；它用背拱，苹果又咕噜一下滑下来。

小刺猬有点着急了。它坐在树下，呼哧呼哧地喘着气。一只小鸟啾啾地飞过来，说：别急呀，想想你身上有什么宝贝？

小刺猬低头一看，呀，自己背上长满了细细的小刺。它把身子往苹果上一靠，轻轻一拱——苹果稳稳地扎在了刺上，再也滚不掉了。

小宝宝，你感觉到了吗？一床云朵一样软的小被子，正轻轻盖过来，把你裹成一个小小的、暖暖的茧。你呀，也有一身属于自己的小宝贝，等着慢慢发现。

小刺猬背着苹果，高高兴兴地往家走。一路上，树叶沙沙沙地为它鼓掌，小蘑菇都探出脑袋来看它。

宝宝呀，等你来到这个世界，也会遇到圆溜溜的小难题。别着急，慢慢想，你身上一定藏着解决它的小本领。

✨ 故事小语
遇到困难不着急，慢慢想办法，总会找到适合自己的方式。就像小刺猬，用身上的刺，背起了最爱的苹果。

The Little Fox's First Snow (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

Deep in the forest lives a red little fox. The old pine tree tells him that when it grows colder, something white and soft called snow will fall from the sky. The little fox has never seen snow. He waits, ears pricked up tall.

At night, the wind blows — whoosh, whoosh — through the treetops. The little fox curls in his den, his tail over his nose. He hears a stream gurgling far away, and an owl hooting — hoo, hoo — but the snow has not come.

He grows sleepy, his eyes drooping shut. Just as he is about to fall asleep, the sky lets down one, then two, soft little white dots, like someone scattering flying feathers.

The little fox leaps up and chases the snowflakes. One lands on his black nose — cool and tingly. One lands on his red tail and melts in a flash. The whole forest turns white and soft and quiet.

Little one, can you feel it? So many firsts in this world are waiting quietly for you, just like this snow. The round moon, like a little night-light, keeps the little fox company, and waits for you too.

The little fox rolls in the snow, leaving a trail of tiny paw prints. He thinks: when you come, I will bring you to see the first snow, and watch our footprints line up into a road that leads home.

Little one, wait slowly, grow slowly. This world's most beautiful sights will be prepared for you, one by one, if you wait with patience.

✨ Story Lesson
The most beautiful sights are prepared for those who wait patiently. Before you even arrive, this world has kept many firsts for you.

The Dandelion's Journey (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

On the meadow grows a dandelion, crowned with tiny white parachutes, crowded together like a soft, glowing cloud.

One day the wind comes and says gently: come, little ones, I will show you the wide world. The parachutes nod — rustle, rustle — and one by one, they let go of their mother's stem.

They drift and float, over golden wheat, over a sparkling stream, over a dozing hillside. Each little parachute is like a tiny, see-through boat.

As evening falls, one parachute settles on a quiet patch of grass and says softly: here, this is the place. It tucks into the soft soil, pulls a thin blanket of earth over itself, and sleeps sweetly.

Little one, can you hear it? A gentle humming drifts in the wind, like a cloud blanket, wrapping the sleeping seed. Mama will stay with you, all along the way, just the same.

When spring comes, that seed will sprout, and grow into a new dandelion, opening many little white parachutes. Then it, too, will send its children off into the wind.

Little one, you are a small seed sleeping inside mama. When the time comes, you will take root in love, sprout, and bloom a flower all your own.

✨ Story Lesson
Leaving is for a better meeting. Every little seed finds, in the wind, a place of its own to take root and grow.

Piglet's Bubble Bath (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

Piglet Hengheng hates bath time. The moment bath time comes, he grunts and wiggles under the bed: I don't want a bath! I don't want a bath!

But today, mama fills the tub with lots and lots of bubbles. White, soft bubbles pile up like a little mountain, and they smell sweet, like oranges.

Hengheng peeks out half his head, and his eyes light up. He climbs in — gurgle, gurgle — and the bubbles rush up — splash, splash — wrapping him into a round, bubbly pig.

He pokes his snout into a puff of bubbles and blows — poof! The bubbles float up and drift around the bathroom. Mama watches and giggles.

Little one, can you hear it? That is papa laughing by the bathroom door, low and warm like distant thunder, a sound that makes everything feel safe.

After the bath, Hengheng is wrapped tight in a big towel — sweet-smelling and soft. He suddenly thinks: a bath isn't so bad after all.

Little one, when you are born, we will give you a sweet bubble bath too. You will find, like Hengheng, that bath time is such a warm, happy thing.

✨ Story Lesson
Some things seem unpleasant until you try them. A bath turns a day's grime into sweet-smelling bubbles.

The Shadow Friend (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

In the evening, the sun slants into the room, and a quiet little shadow appears on the wall. It moves with the light, slowly, like a gentle river, rippling soft.

The little shadow makes no sound and never fusses. In the daytime it hides under your feet; at evening it climbs the wall and becomes a little black cat, a small hill, a fish that swims. The wind slips through the window crack — whoosh — teasing it.

It keeps the potted plant company, and the cup on the table, and mama as she folds clothes. When mama raises a hand, the shadow raises a hand; when mama lowers her head, the shadow lowers its head too. The clothes settle with a soft rustle.

The sky darkens bit by bit. The little shadow fades, and at last says softly: I am going home now. Tomorrow evening, I will come to keep you company again.

Little one, can you feel it? The stars join hands into a tiny cradle and rock you gently, like the little shadow, keeping watch over you in silence.

At night the little shadow is gone. But it has not gone far — it hides behind the light, and when the sun returns, it slips quietly back onto the wall.

Little one, there is a kind of company that is quiet. It says nothing, yet is always there — like a shadow, like everyone at home waiting for you to be born.

✨ Story Lesson
Some company is quiet; it says nothing, yet is always there. Like a shadow, like the family waiting for you to be born.

The Little Hedgehog's Apple (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

Autumn has come, and the woods are covered in golden leaves. Under a tree, the little hedgehog finds a big, red apple — sweet-smelling and shiny.

He wants to carry it home, but the apple is so round. He pushes it with his paws, and it rolls away — roll, roll. He nudges it with his back, and it slips off — plop.

The little hedgehog grows a little worried. He sits under the tree, puffing — huff, huff. A little bird flits down — tweet, tweet — and says: don't worry, what treasure do you have on your back?

The hedgehog looks down. Oh! His back is covered with fine little quills. He leans against the apple and gives a little nudge — and the apple sticks firmly to his quills, never rolling away again.

Little one, can you feel it? A cloud-soft blanket is tucking over you, wrapping you into a small, warm cocoon. You, too, have little treasures of your own, waiting to be discovered.

The little hedgehog carries the apple home, happy as can be. Along the way, the leaves rustle, clapping for him, and the little mushrooms poke their heads out to watch.

Little one, when you arrive, you will meet round little problems too. Don't rush — think slowly, and you will find the little skill hidden in you to solve them.

✨ Story Lesson
When you meet a hard problem, do not rush — think slowly and you will find your own way. Like the hedgehog, who used his quills to carry his favorite apple.

月亮妈妈的哼歌（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

夜空里，月亮妈妈轻轻守着一团暖暖的小光，那光里住着还没有出生的小宝宝。她弯下腰，轻声问：小宝贝，你猜「我是谁」呀？

风从窗边溜过来，把夜晚的声音都染上了颜色——蛐蛐蛐蛐，是绿色的小提琴；远处的钟摆咯噔咯噔，是金色的摇椅在慢慢摇。

月亮妈妈飘过人家的窗台，看见会发光的夜灯、软软的毛毯、还有滴答走动的小钟。这些平平常常的物件，原来都悄悄藏着家人的爱。

她忽然很想唱歌。于是轻轻哼了起来——哼歌像一朵云朵做的被子，把小宝宝软软地裹住，哼歌里还藏着摇篮曲的节拍，一下，又一下。

小宝宝，你听见了吗？那阵温柔的哼歌声，是妈妈在替月亮妈妈和你说悄悄话呢。叮铃铃，连风铃也踮起脚，来给这首歌伴唱。

月亮妈妈忽然明白了：原来「我是谁」并不重要，重要的是，有这么多声音和颜色，都在陪着你慢慢长大。

早安，小宝宝，今天的世界亮晶晶的。等你准备好了，外面的风会很软，月光会很暖，我们都在轻轻地，等你。

✨ 故事小语
爱是有声音也有颜色的，每一份温柔都在悄悄告诉你：你被好好地爱着，慢慢长大就好。

The Little Alarm Clock's Lullaby (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

On the windowsill sat a tiny alarm clock named Ding-dang, who loved nothing more than singing to the baby still asleep in Mama's tummy. Tonight he had a wonderful secret to share.

He whispered a soft tune, hush, hush, and listened. From the next room came Daddy's boom-boom snore, low and safe like distant thunder. Ding-dong, Ding-dang tapped gently, keeping the beat.

Grandma's hands were knitting by the lamp, click-clack, click-clack, weaving warm little socks. The way home was short, but it was full of sounds that meant 'you are loved.'

Ding-dang leaned close to the round little tummy and said in his tiny ticking voice: little one, can you hear it? That boom-boom, boom-boom — that is Mama's heartbeat, the very first song written just for you.

He discovered something wonderful: every family sound was a lullaby. The hum of the light, the creak of the floor, even the quiet — all of it was the world saying welcome.

So he sang his goodnight the only way he knew: ding-dong, time to sleep. And the baby, still small and unseen, listened and grew safe and sound in the warm, singing dark.

✨ Story Lesson
Every little sound of a family is a kind of lullaby — love is all around you, even before you are born.

小闹钟叮当的午夜乐队（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

窗台上的小闹钟叮当，最爱在深夜办一场小小的音乐会。它轻轻晃着身体：叮咚，该睡啦。

淅淅沥沥，窗外下起了细细的小雨，像有人在轻轻敲着小鼓。叮当竖起耳朵，觉得这雨声真好听。

一只小猫跳上窗台，咕咕地打着呼噜，像一把小小的暖壶。叮当和它一唱一和，雨声、呼噜声，正好凑成一支午夜乐队。

扑棱扑棱，窗外的树叶也被吵醒了，抖落几颗亮晶晶的雨珠。叮当悄悄把闹钟的指针拨慢一点点，好让这场音乐会多响一会儿。

小宝宝，你听到了吗？这些轻轻的声音，都是夜里的小精灵在给你唱摇篮曲呢。

后来雨停了，月亮探出头，把银色的光铺在叮当身上。它打了个小小的哈欠：晚安，小宝宝，月亮陪着你。

✨ 故事小语
夜晚不是黑黑的，它藏着许多轻轻的声音陪着你；安心睡吧，世界很温柔。

The Seed Who Counted the Stars (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

Deep in Mama's garden, a tiny seed named Sprout waited under the warm soil. She was patient, and she had a happy little secret.

Above her, the earth turned slowly — ding-dong went a faraway bell, and fizz went the little bubbles in the dew. The whole round world was spinning, carrying her like a cradle.

Sprout listened to the Sahara's starry sky through the dark: lub-dub, lub-dub, that was Mama's heartbeat, steady as a drum. 'Just a little longer,' Sprout whispered to the baby above.

She dreamed she grew into a small tree whose leaves were made of the coral reef's colors, and whose shade was the Grand Canyon's quiet book. The baby in the tummy would one day read it, page by page.

When morning came, a single drop of rain tapped her shell: tick, tick. Sprout smiled. She was exactly where she needed to be.

Little one, grow slow and safe. The stars are counting you, and the garden is waiting — good night, tiny traveler, good night.

✨ Story Lesson
Good things grow slowly and gently — just like you, little one, taking your time in a world full of wonder.

小星星暖暖的星光摇篮（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

圆滚滚的小星星暖暖，最爱眨着眼睛在夜空里散步。今晚，它想给还没出生的小宝宝编一个摇篮。

叮咚叮咚，暖暖敲了敲云朵的门，请来一朵软软的云当摇篮底。滴滴答答，远处的小溪也送来一捧清凉的月光，铺在云上当作小被子。

一只小猫蹲在屋檐下，喵喵地仰头看，好像也想来坐坐。暖暖笑着邀它一起，星光就把它们轻轻圈成一个小小的圆。

星星们手拉手，围成一个温暖的摇篮，慢慢摇着、摇着，像在哼一首没有词的歌。小宝宝，你感觉到那轻轻的摇晃了吗？

暖暖凑近妈妈的肚子，小声说：我陪着你呀。不管黑夜多长，总有一颗小星星，专门为你亮着。

晚安，小宝宝，月亮陪着你。等你准备好了，这艘星光摇篮，会摇着你，去看世界上所有软软的梦。

✨ 故事小语
你从来都不是一个人，温柔的光和陪伴一直都在，安安心心地长大就好。

Conch Shell's Gentle Treasure (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

In the deep-sea seagrass forest lived a little white conch named Swirl, quiet and full of curiosity. Tonight she set out to find a treasure.

She drifted along, sway-sway, sway-sway, held by the soft current like a baby in gentle arms. Ding-dong, a far bell-fish rang, and hush, the water hushed her name.

Swirl met a slow turtle, a napping cat-fish, and a lamb-soft cloud of bubbles. Each one gave her a tiny gift: a blue, a hum, a warm patch of light.

She gathered them into a blanket of color — the orange of dusk, the gold of wheat, the blue of the sea — and carried it all the way to Mama's tummy.

There she tucked it around the baby: the warmth of a blanket, a cloud-soft cocoon. Little one, can you feel how many friends helped make this for you?

Swirl curled at the edge of the wave and whispered: treasure found. Good night, little one, the sea is rocking you, safe and sound.

云朵朵朵和心跳的小鼓（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

云端小精灵朵朵，最爱飘呀飘去照顾每一个人。今晚，她飘到妈妈肚子边，听见里面有一面小小的鼓。

呜呜，晚风轻轻吹过；滴答滴答，那是妈妈心跳的小鼓，一下一下，稳稳地敲着。朵朵把耳朵贴上去，觉得又暖又安心。

沙沙沙，窗外的树也睡着了，叶子互相蹭着，像在说晚安。朵朵把一捧云朵轻轻盖在妈妈肚子上，像盖一床软软的被子。

小宝宝，你听见那面小鼓了吗？咚、咚、咚，那是妈妈在替全世界跟你说：我在呢，别急。

朵朵拉着一缕月光，在妈妈肚子上织了个小摇篮。她想：等宝宝出来的那天，这面小鼓会一直响，一直陪着他。

晚安，小宝宝，月亮陪着你。你被好好地抱着，慢慢地、甜甜地，长大就好。

Brown Bear's Shared Lunch (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

Little brown bear An-an lived in a cozy forest hollow, soft and slow and full of calm. One quiet evening he packed a tiny shared lunch.

He padded along, pat-pat, pat-pat, humming a purr of a tune. A grandmother squirrel offered him a seat; an old tortoise shared a crust of warm bread. Kindness, he learned, was just passing things along.

An-an thought of the baby in Mama's tummy and wanted to share something too. He gathered a cradle of stars — huff... lu... he blew them gently upward, where they held hands and rocked.

The stars sang a wordless song, dum-dum, dum-dum, like a lullaby without words. Little one, can you hear the gentle rocking? That is the whole forest saying you belong.

He left the shared lunch by the moonlit path: a crumb of bread, a whisper of fur, a handful of starlight. Someone tiny would find it soon.

Good night, little one. The world is kind, and you are already held in more love than you can yet know.

小熊安安和会数数的向日葵（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

小熊安安慢吞吞地走出树洞，呼……噜……他要到暖洋洋的白天里去散散步。草叶上挂着露珠，簌簌地落下来。

扑通，一颗松果掉进小水坑，荡开一圈圈温柔的波纹。安安蹲下来看，觉得这水坑像一面小镜子，照出蓝蓝的天。

前面是一大片向日葵田。安安数啊数，发现每朵花里的籽都排成转圈圈的小路——一枚、两枚、三枚、五枚，越数越有意思。原来向日葵也会数数呀。

风轻轻吹，滴答滴答，是远处屋檐下妈妈在哼歌。那哼歌顺着阳光飘过来，软软地裹住安安的耳朵。小宝宝，你听见了吗？那是妈妈在替整个世界，给你唱早安。

安安把一片向日葵叶子顶在头上，慢吞吞地往回走。暖阳晒着背，他满足地舒了口气：今天的世界，亮晶晶的，真好。

等你准备好了，小宝宝，这些会数数的花、会唱歌的风，都会在门外，轻轻地等你。

✨ 故事小语
世界很大很奇妙，慢慢走、慢慢看，每一件小东西里都藏着让人惊喜的秩序与爱。

Mama Moon and the Spinning World (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

Guardian Mama Moon floated in the night sky, gentle and full of wisdom. She leaned down to the round little tummy and said: sleep safe, Mama is here.

By daylight she took the baby on a quiet journey. Tick-tack went the little clock of the earth as it turned; tweet sang a small bird who had never flown, only practiced. Pat-pat went tiny feet exploring a sunlit path.

They visited the stone statues' dream on Easter Island, where old stone faces smiled in their sleep. They floated inside a dictionary, where every word was a small warm lamp lighting the way home.

And all along, beneath it all, came the drumbeat of a heartbeat — thump, thump, Mama's own. Little one, can you hear it? The whole spinning world keeps time with that same gentle drum.

They shared a peaceful lunch on a cloud: a crumb of light, a sip of blue. The baby, still unseen, grew calm and curious in the warm, turning day.

When the sun leaned low, Mama Moon tucked the light around the tummy and whispered: you are held by the whole round world. Sleep safe, little one, sleep safe.

✨ Story Lesson
The world turns with a steady, loving rhythm — and that same rhythm beats inside you, from the very first day.

小种子芽芽和书签的故事（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

妈妈的花园里，一粒小种子芽芽裹着暖暖的土，耐心地等着自己发芽。它轻轻说：再等等，就快了。

啪嗒啪嗒，一只小蜗牛爬过书页，留下亮亮的水痕。芽芽从土里望出去，看见一张书签正躺在故事书里，静静看着一行行字。

书签悄悄告诉芽芽：我见过一个不会写字的男孩，他用画来讲故事；我也见过一个小朋友，把睡前故事的温度，一页页折成了小船。滴滴答答，是雨点在窗外给故事打拍子。

喵喵，隔壁的小猫凑过来看书，把毛茸茸的尾巴当书签夹了进去。芽芽忍不住笑了：原来编故事的快乐，连小猫也想分一口。

圆圆的月亮升起来，安安静静陪着这一页一页的世界。月光守候：它像一盏小夜灯，把影子照得软软的，好让每个故事都睡得安稳。

晚安，小宝宝，月亮陪着你。等你出来了，芽芽也发芽了，我们一起，把今天的故事，讲给你听。

✨ 故事小语
每一个故事都像一粒种子，被温柔地讲出来，就会在心里悄悄发芽。

Dewdrop Postman's Goodnight Letter (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

On a petal at dawn lived a lively little dewdrop named Lulu, full of questions. Each morning she cried: a letter's here!

Tonight her letters were all about home. Flutter went the curtain as Daddy snored a low, rumbling tune — boom, boom, like a far-off train. Ding-dong, the dinner bell rang, and stories tumbled out between bites of warm rice.

Mama's catchphrase floated through the house like a hug: 'careful, sweet, I'm right here.' Lulu folded it into a tiny envelope and tucked it by the baby's ear.

She gathered them all into one warm embrace — a ring of light like open arms, holding the little one steady, as if in a soft nest. Little one, can you feel how many hands wrote this letter just for you?

Whisper, whisper, the house grew quiet. Lulu sealed the goodnight kiss with a drop of moonlight and slid it under the door of the tummy.

Good night, little one. The mail of love is delivered every day, and you are its favorite address.

小海螺小旋旋的光之旅行（胎教期）
🇨🇳 中文
— ✿ —

深海的海草森林里，小海螺小旋旋睁着亮亮的小眼睛，对什么都好奇。今晚，它想看看光是怎么玩耍的。

呼呼，水流推着它慢慢飘；咕嘟咕嘟，气泡像一串小灯笼从脚边升起来。小旋旋跟着光斑，从海草的缝里钻了出去。

淅淅沥沥，月光洒在水面上，被波浪揉成了会跳舞的小影子。墙上映出摇摇摆摆的光，像在和它捉迷藏。小旋旋看呆了：原来光也会玩游戏呀。

远远地，传来爸爸低沉温柔的笑声，像春天的雷声一样让人安心。小旋旋把笑声装进贝壳里，想留着给还没出生的小宝宝听。

原来是这样呀……小旋旋轻轻叹了口气。它忽然明白，光的游戏背后，是整片温柔的世界在陪着它。

晚安，小宝宝，月亮陪着你。等你准备好了，小旋旋带你，去看会跳舞的光，和爸爸藏在风里的笑声。

✨ 故事小语
世界很大，奇妙很多，但你永远被温柔的光和笑声包围着，慢慢探索就好。

Little Bird's Aurora Song (Prenatal)
🇬🇧 EN
— ✿ —

Under the eaves lived a little bird named Chirp who sang lullabies, gentle and sweet. Tweet, tweet, sleep now, she would say to the round little tummy below.

One night Chirp flew up to see the dance of the aurora — ruff went her wings, and wiggle went the green and violet ribbons across the sky. It was the world's own lullaby, painted high.

Ding-dong, a far ice crystal rang, clear as a bell. Chirp looked down at Lake Baikal's clarity, where the water held the stars like a second sky, and at the Sahara's starry hush, where the sand kept every ancient secret.

She remembered the earth's slow rotation, turning all these wonders past the baby's unseen window. Moonlight watched: a round moon like a little night-light, quietly keeping the baby company, softening every shadow.

Chirp folded a scrap of aurora into her song and dropped it, soft as a feather, onto Mama's tummy. Little one, can you hear the colors? They are singing you a goodnight.

Good night, little one. The whole wide world is rehearsing its welcome — and the moon is right there, smiling, just for you.

✨ Story Lesson
The world is full of quiet wonders waiting for you — and you are watched over by love, even before you arrive.
